Shadow QSVT provides a systematic route to reduce the quantum resources required by standard QSVT, which constructs a unitary block-encoding of $f(H)$. It uses structure in the input state and observables, together with the fact that many applications require only observable estimates rather than synthesizing the full unitary.
  We present three algorithms that exploit structure in the initial state and observables to reduce quantum circuit depth. First, we develop a state-aware QSVT algorithm that prepares the target state with low circuit depth when the Krylov subspace associated with $H$ and $|ψ\rangle$ is low-dimensional or admits an accurate low-dimensional approximation. Second, we introduce an observable-aware Shadow QSVT algorithm that combines a new observable-aware Krylov subspace with history states to further reduce circuit depth and gate complexity. Finally, we develop Classical Shadow QSVT, which constructs a classical representation from $H$, $f$, and $|ψ\rangle$ without prior knowledge of the observables or explicit preparation of the target state proportional to $f(H)|ψ\rangle$. This representation enables estimation of the target quantities for observables specified after the quantum computation. Together, these three algorithms provide tools for reducing the circuit depth of QSVT-based computations across a range of settings.

</details>


### [Non-Markovian dissipation as a resource for quantum reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40172)

Luca Nigro、Francesco Monzani、Enrico Prati

量子机器学习 · 开放量子系统 · 量子控制

**中文摘要**

开放系统动力学的控制为利用量子信息处理序列任务提供了强大机制。虽然量子储备池计算通常依赖马尔可夫耗散来处理序列数据，非马尔可夫记忆效应的计算作用仍很大程度上未被探索。我们引入一个量子储备池计算框架，其中非马尔可夫性使用分数阶导数被显式建模和调控。通过采用分数阶时间从属，我们生成可调的重尾弛豫动力学，这些动力学支配系统与其环境之间的信息回流。非马尔可夫信息回流调节系统的整体时间保持能力，使短期线性记忆容量最大化。在一个最优运行区间内，分数阶非马尔可夫性将储备池记忆重新分配到近期输入，以牺牲长程时间保持为代价，提高短期延迟下的线性记忆容量和非线性预测精度。系统-环境相互作用被证明不仅是量子储备池计算的一个要求，而且是一种主动资源，将记忆保持直接嵌入量子演化之中。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

摘要报告，通过分数阶导数显式建模和调控非马尔可夫性，并利用分数阶时间从属产生可调的重尾弛豫动力学，可调节系统与环境间的信息回流和整体时间保持能力；在最优运行区间内，该机制将储备池记忆偏向近期输入，提高短期延迟下的线性记忆容量与非线性预测精度，但牺牲长程时间保持。适用范围为量子储备池计算处理序列数据；上述效果来自摘要作者报告，尚不能视为已独立验证的普适结论。

**创新点**

- 提出一个使用分数阶导数显式建模并调控非马尔可夫性的量子储备池计算框架。
- 通过分数阶时间从属生成可调的重尾弛豫动力学，以调控系统与环境之间的信息回流。
- 表明系统-环境相互作用可作为主动资源，将记忆保持直接嵌入量子演化，并体现短期与长程记忆保持之间的权衡。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

The control of open-system dynamics provides a powerful mechanism for using quantum information to process sequential tasks. While quantum reservoir computing typically relies on Markovian dissipation to process sequential data, the computational role of non-Markovian memory effects remains largely unexplored. We introduce a framework for quantum reservoir computing where non-Markovianity is explicitly modeled and regulated using fractional derivatives. By employing fractional time subordination, we generate tunable, heavy-tailed relaxation dynamics that govern the information backflow between the system and its environment. Non-Markovian information backflow regulates the overall temporal retention of the system, maximizing short-term linear memory capacity. Within an optimal operating regime, fractional non-Markovianity redistributes the reservoir memory towards recent inputs, improving both linear memory capacity and nonlinear prediction accuracy at short delays, at the expense of long-range temporal retention. System-environment interaction proves being not merely a requirement for quantum reservoir computing, but an active resource that embeds memory retention directly into the quantum evolution.

</details>


### [Practical fermionic shadows enabled by improved sample-complexity bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40173)

Maxwell West、Su Yeon Chang、Luke Coffman、Martin Larocca、M. Cerezo

量子测量 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

经典影子层析被广泛推崇为提供了一类以多项式规模样本复杂度从量子系统中提取信息的方法。然而，在拥有数百个量子比特量级的量子计算机的当前时代，多项式缩放仍可能高得难以承受。因此，在实际中强烈需要获得尽可能紧的样本复杂度上界。这里我们针对费米子（匹配门）影子处理这一问题。对于 Majorana 度数为 \(2k\) 的任意可观测量 \(O\)，我们将此前已知的样本复杂度上界 \(\mathcal{O}(n^{2k}\|O\|_\infty^2)\) 改进为 \(\mathcal{O}(n^{k}\|O\|_\infty^2)\)，该界是渐近紧的。例如，对于估计一条开放的费米子 50 格点 Hubbard 链的每模式能量，其中跃迁和在位强度分别由 \(t=1\)、\(V=4\) 给出，并且目标加性精度为 0.1，这将所需采样次数从 \(\sim 10^9\) 降至 \(\sim 10^5\)。也就是说，新上界将所需采样次数减少约 \(99.98\%\)。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者推导出 matchgate 影子下同质费米子度数可观测量可学习性的渐近最优样本复杂度上界，并认为该结果在已有实验探测数百量子比特的区间具有实际意义；证明高度技术化，作者怀疑界中的 3/2 因子是证明策略的产物，数值实验指向最优常数为 1。硬件实现方面，matchgate 3-设计的电路深度仍是难点：一维近邻架构上亚线性深度系综不能形成 matchgate 3-设计，而全连接架构上可由匹配门与 Clifford 群交集中的系综以最优对数深度形成；因此若近期硬件支持全连接相互作用，结合本文的最优样本复杂度，将显著提高有用费米子影子层析的前景，囚禁离子和中性原子处理器可能提供这种连通性。上述结论主要适用于 matchgate 影子框架及相应架构假设，材料未报告实验实现验证。

**创新点**

- 作者报告将任意 Majorana 度数 \(2k\) 可观测量的样本复杂度上界从 \(\mathcal{O}(n^{2k}\|O\|_\infty^2)\) 改进为渐近紧的 \(\mathcal{O}(n^{k}\|O\|_\infty^2)\)。
- 作者以开放 50 格点费米子 Hubbard 链每模式能量估计为例，展示所需采样次数可从约 \(10^9\) 降至约 \(10^5\)，约减少 \(99.98\%\)。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Classical shadow tomography is widely touted as supplying a family of methods for extracting information from quantum systems with polynomially scaling sample-complexities. In the current era of quantum computers possessing on the order of hundreds of qubits, however, polynomial scaling can nonetheless be prohibitive. Thus, there is a strong practical need for obtaining sample-complexity bounds which are as tight as possible. Here we address this in the case of fermionic (matchgate) shadows. For an arbitrary observable $O$ of Majorana degree $2k$, we improve the previously known sample-complexity bound of $\mathcal{O}(n^{2k}\|O\|_\infty^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n^{k}\|O\|_\infty^2)$, which is asymptotically tight. For example, for the estimation of the energy per mode of an open fermionic 50-site Hubbard chain with hopping and on site strenghts respectively given by $t=1$, $V=4$, and for a target additive precision of 0.1, this reduces the number of required shots from $\sim 10^9$ to $\sim 10^5$. That is, the new bound reduces the required number of shots by approximately $99.98\%$.

</details>


### [Dissipation accelerates quantum and classical simulation of open-system dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40174)

Armando Angrisani、Ricard Puig、Yanting Teng、Zoë Holmes

量子模拟 · 开放量子系统

**中文摘要**

模拟开放量子系统揭示环境耦合如何塑造弛豫、激发输运以及量子关联的动力学。在量子硬件上，耗散通道会增加操作，看似可能提高成本。然而，我们表明，一大类Pauli噪声（包括去极化）可以通过指数抑制Heisenberg演化的可观测量中的高权重分量，使量子模拟和经典模拟都变得更容易。对于有界度Lindblad动力学，在估计局部可观测量时，这允许Trotter步长与系统尺寸无关。将这种压缩与Richardson外推结合，我们给出了关于传播可观测量2-范数的经典和量子误差界。这些结果为随机输入态，或作用于固定态和控制的随机Hamiltonian，以及（时序外）关联函数，提供高概率的期望值保证。具体而言，我们表明，在时间$t$处局部可观测量的期望值可以以精度$\varepsilon$估计，使用最大深度为$O\!\left(\left[1+\left(t/\gamma\right)^{3/2}\right]\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\right)$的电路，且与系统尺寸$n$无关。在经典方面，我们表明，对于每个固定$\gamma>0$，稀疏Pauli传播的运行时间为$n$、$t$和$1/\varepsilon$的多项式，在弱耗散极限下多项式次数为$O(1/\gamma)$。我们的量子与经典上界之间的差距为耗散减弱时出现显著多项式量子优势留下了空间，我们的数值结果进一步支持这一观察。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者结论认为，耗散通过抑制演化可观测量中的高权重Pauli分量，可使开放系统动力学在量子与经典硬件上更易模拟；在其证明覆盖的一类局域耗散（要求局域耗散器阻尼其配对相互作用区域上每个非恒等Pauli方向，去极化满足）及有界度Lindblad动力学下，局部可观测量的估计可用与系统尺寸无关的Trotter步长，固定$\gamma>0$时稀疏Pauli传播为多项式时间。量子和经典上界间的差距提示耗散减弱时可能存在显著多项式量子优势，但作者指出这仍需针对具体硬件架构的端到端资源分析；且结果受限于特定耗散类，Z退相位、关联库和振幅阻尼等未覆盖。

**创新点**

- 证明包括去极化的一类Pauli噪声可通过指数抑制Heisenberg演化可观测量中的高权重分量，降低量子与经典模拟成本。
- 对局部可观测量给出与系统尺寸$n$无关的最大电路深度$O\!\left(\left[1+\left(t/\gamma\right)^{3/2}\right]\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\right)$。
- 结合Richardson外推给出基于传播可观测量2-范数的经典与量子误差界，并证明固定$\gamma>0$时稀疏Pauli传播关于$n$、$t$和$1/\varepsilon$为多项式时间。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Simulating open quantum systems reveals how environmental coupling shapes relaxation, excitation transport, and the dynamics of quantum correlations. On quantum hardware, dissipative channels add operations and might seem to increase cost. However, we show that a broad class of Pauli noise, including depolarization, can ease quantum and classical simulation by exponentially suppressing high-weight components of Heisenberg-evolved observables. For bounded-degree Lindblad dynamics, this permits system-size-independent Trotter steps when estimating local observables. Combining this compression with Richardson extrapolation, we provide classical and quantum error bounds with respect to the 2-norm of the propagated observable. These give high-probability expectation-value guarantees for random input states or random Hamiltonians acting on a fixed state and control (out-of-time-order) correlators. Concretely, we show that expectation values of local observables at time $t$ can be estimated to accuracy $\varepsilon$ using circuits of maximum depth $O\!\left(\left[1+\left(t/γ\right)^{3/2}\right]\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\right)$, independent of the system size $n$. Classically, we show that sparse Pauli propagation runs in time polynomial in $n$, $t$ and $1/\varepsilon$ for every fixed $γ>0$, with polynomial degree $O(1/γ)$ in the weak dissipation limit. The gap between our quantum and classical upper bounds leaves room for a substantial polynomial quantum advantage as dissipation weakens, an observation further supported by our numerical results.

</details>


### [Breaking the Multiplicative Overhead in Quantum Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40179)

Junxiang Huang、Chenyang Li、Lu-Fan Zhang、Yusen Wu、Yukun Zhang

量子算法 · 量子复杂性 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

冯·诺依曼熵、Tsallis 熵和 Rényi 熵是量子信息的基本度量。一种常见的估计策略将谱变换的最坏情况成本与统计读出的最坏情况成本相乘，从而与查询下界之间存在差距。我们通过使用局部归一化和精度分配的多级算法来降低这一开销，同时变时间估计考虑了到达昂贵谱测试的概率。在受控纯化访问及其逆的条件下，我们针对加性误差 ε、秩上界 R 和固定阶 α 得到界。Tsallis 熵估计具有近最优且与秩无关的查询复杂度 \widetilde O(ε^{-1/(α-1)})（1<α<2），前提是允许的维度和秩能容纳下界实例；对于 α≥2 则为 \widetilde O(1/ε)，其中 \widetilde O 抑制对数因子。前者节省了因子 1/ε；后者将已知的整数阶标度推广到非整数阶。我们还将冯·诺依曼熵的界从 \widetilde O(R/ε^2) 改进到 \widetilde O(R/ε)，并对非整数 Rényi 阶 1<α<3 得到 \widetilde O(R/ε)，在其他阶数下还有进一步的界。我们的函数估计定理将全局乘积替换为按函数幅度和谱质量加权的局部成本之和。我们进一步使用 \widetilde O(R/ε) 次查询估计固定对数矩和熵方差，从而高效获取进入有限块长量子压缩和纯态纠缠转换的涨落参数。更广泛地说，我们的框架超越熵，扩展到一大类密度矩阵泛函，为量子谱估计中的最优查询复杂度提供系统方法。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

该工作的核心结论是：先进行最坏情况谱变换再进行最坏情况读出的乘性开销并非量子熵估计的根本限制。由于分辨率尺度、局部函数幅度和谱质量由同一组特征值决定，作者在整个构造中保留这种依赖关系，用局部成本之和替代全局乘积；局部归一化带来与秩无关的 Tsallis 熵改进，生存敏感估计为冯·诺依曼熵提供额外节省，而对 Rényi 熵，矩尺度估计进一步约束所选质量，生存概率计入到达该质量的成本。由此得到的 Tsallis 界对每个大于 1 的固定阶近最优：当允许的秩能容纳困难实例时，1<α<2 为 \widetilde Θ(ε^{-1/(α-1)})，α≥2 为 \widetilde Θ(1/ε)。对于冯·诺依曼熵和 Rényi 熵，新界改进了现有算法，但未解决所有秩–精度联合权衡；冯·诺依曼熵的 \widetilde O(R/ε) 与 \widetilde Ω(R/√ε+1/ε) 之间仍有差距，相应的低秩 Tsallis 和非整数阶 Rényi 情形仍是相关的开放问题。该框架也适用于更广泛的一类标量谱统计，并在可有效近似归一化局部剖面且被丢弃贡献受控时，将估计成本表示为局部量而非独立优化的全局变换。

**创新点**

- 提出使用局部归一化和精度分配的多级算法，并用变时间估计计入到达昂贵谱测试的概率，以降低谱变换与统计读出的乘性开销。
- 提出函数估计定理，将全局乘积替换为按函数幅度和谱质量加权的局部成本之和。
- 给出 Tsallis 熵的近最优秩无关查询界，并改进冯·诺依曼熵、非整数阶 Rényi 熵以及固定对数矩和熵方差的查询复杂度界。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

The von Neumann, Tsallis, and Rényi entropies are fundamental measures of quantum information. A common estimation strategy multiplies the worst-case costs of spectral transformation and statistical readout, leaving gaps to query lower bounds. We reduce this overhead with multi-level algorithms that use local normalization and precision allocation, while variable-time estimation accounts for the probability of reaching expensive spectral tests. With controlled purified access and its inverse, we obtain bounds for additive error $\varepsilon$, rank upper bound $R$, and fixed order $α$. Tsallis entropy estimation has near-optimal rank-independent query complexity $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-1/(α-1)})$ for $1<α<2$, when the allowed dimension and rank accommodate the lower-bound instances, and $\widetilde O(1/\varepsilon)$ for $α\ge2$, where $\widetilde O$ suppresses logarithmic factors. The first saves a factor $1/\varepsilon$; the second extends known integer-order scaling to noninteger orders. We also improve the von Neumann entropy bound from $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon^2)$ to $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon)$ and obtain $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon)$ for noninteger Rényi orders $1<α<3$, with further bounds at other orders. Our functional-estimation theorem replaces the global product by a sum of local costs weighted by function magnitudes and spectral masses. We further estimate fixed logarithmic moments and entropy variance using $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon)$ queries, providing efficient access to fluctuation parameters that enter finite-blocklength quantum compression and pure-state entanglement conversion. More broadly, our framework extends beyond entropy to a broad class of density-matrix functionals, offering a systematic approach toward optimal query complexity in quantum spectral estimation.

</details>


### [Improved Quantum Query Bounds for Boolean Matrix Product Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40180)

Amin Shiraz Gilani、François Le Gall、Xingyu Zhou

量子复杂性 · 量子算法

**中文摘要**

我们证明了布尔矩阵乘积验证（$\mathsf{BMPV}$）量子查询复杂度的首个非平凡上界，回答了量子查询复杂度中的一个长期未决问题。对于 $n\times n$ 矩阵，我们的上界为 $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$，改进了 Buhrman 和 Špalek（SODA 2006）利用 Grover 搜索得到的标准 $O(n^{3/2})$ 上界。我们通过给出一个 $\Omega(n^{5/4})$ 下界来补充这一结果，该下界改进了 Childs、Kimmel 和 Kothari（ESA 2012）此前已知最好的 $\widetilde\Omega(n^{19/18})$ 下界。

我们的方法核心是与正交向量（$\mathsf{OV}$）问题的联系：该问题询问一个由 $n$ 个 $n$ 维布尔向量组成的有索引列表是否包含两个支撑集不相交的向量。特别地，我们证明了 $\mathsf{OV}$ 与 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 之间的等价关系，并为 $\mathsf{OV}$ 建立了上述界。我们还证明了 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 的一个变体的紧界 $\widetilde\Theta(n^{3/2})$，该变体询问乘积是否包含一个给定行向量。综合这些结果，可推出判定一个图半径是否至多二与判定其直径是否至多二这两个问题的量子查询复杂度之间存在多项式分离。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

作者在摘要中报告，对 $n\times n$ 布尔矩阵乘积验证给出首个非平凡量子查询上界 $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$ 和 $\Omega(n^{5/4})$ 下界；这些结果通过 $\mathsf{OV}$ 与 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 的等价关系得到，并给出 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 一个变体的紧界 $\widetilde\Theta(n^{3/2})$，由此推出图半径至多二与直径至多二的量子查询复杂度之间存在多项式分离。上述结论的适用范围限于摘要所述问题与查询复杂度模型；未提供正文结论章节。

**创新点**

- 作者报告给出 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 的首个非平凡量子查询上界 $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$，改进了 Buhrman 和 Špalek 的 $O(n^{3/2})$ 上界。
- 作者建立 $\mathsf{OV}$ 与 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 之间的等价关系，并将上述上下界用于 $\mathsf{OV}$。
- 作者证明 $\mathsf{BMPV}$ 一个变体的紧界 $\widetilde\Theta(n^{3/2})$，并据此得到图半径至多二与直径至多二的量子查询复杂度之间的多项式分离。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

We prove the first non-trivial upper bound for the quantum query complexity of Boolean Matrix Product Verification ($\mathsf{BMPV}$), answering a longstanding open question in quantum query complexity. For $n\times n$ matrices, our upper bound is $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$, improving on the standard $O(n^{3/2})$ bound obtained using Grover search by Buhrman and Špalek (SODA 2006). We complement this result by showing an $Ω(n^{5/4})$ lower bound, which improves over the previous best known lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(n^{19/18})$ by Childs, Kimmel, and Kothari (ESA 2012).
  Our approach centers on a connection with Orthogonal Vectors ($\mathsf{OV}$), which asks whether an indexed list of $n$ Boolean vectors of dimension $n$ contains two vectors with disjoint supports. In particular, we prove equivalences between $\mathsf{OV}$ and $\mathsf{BMPV}$ and establish the above bounds for $\mathsf{OV}$. We also prove a tight $\widetilde Θ(n^{3/2})$ bound for a variant of $\mathsf{BMPV}$ that asks whether the product contains a given row vector. Together, these results imply a polynomial separation between the quantum query complexities of deciding whether a graph has radius at most two and whether it has diameter at most two.

</details>


### [A mixing time method for estimating the sample complexity of quantum state discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40182)

Juntai Zhou、Felix Leditzky

量子信息理论 · 量子算法 · 量子测量

**中文摘要**

我们开发了一种混合时间方法，用于估计量子态分辨的样本复杂度。我们首先考虑几何均匀纯态系综的最小误差分辨，并证明其样本复杂度具有由量子齐次混合时间 [George et al., 2026] 和广义 Dobrushin 系数的量子版本 [Wolfer, 2020] 给出的紧估计。当生成群 $G$ 与生成态的稳定子子群 $H$ 构成 Gelfand 对时，该量子混合时间进一步约化为经典混合时间。在这种情况下，广义 Dobrushin 系数可以完全由交换 Hecke 代数 $\operatorname{End}_G(\mathbb C[G/H])$ 的表示论量表达。特别地，该方法将学习量子优惠券收集态 [Arunachalam et al., 2020] 和学习相位态的样本复杂度估计约化为经典混合时间问题。我们应用该框架回答了 [Alrabiah et al., 2026] 中关于在 $\mathbb F_q$ 上学习度数为 $d$ 的相位态的公开问题，以及 [Arunachalam et al., 2023] 中关于在 $\mathbb Z_q$ 上的广义布尔相位态的公开问题。该框架也适用于超图态系综，给出完全用超图数据表达的估计，并恢复 [Montanaro and Shao, 2022] 中对图态系综的估计。最后，我们将讨论扩展到具有均匀先验的任意混合态系综，证明由量子弱混合时间给出的最小误差分辨样本复杂度的夹逼界，并由 Dobrushin 型系数给出 [D'Ariano et al., 2005] 中极小极大分辨样本复杂度的紧估计。我们还讨论了强化数据处理不等式方法 [Gao and Rouz{é}, 2022]，并给出以强化数据处理不等式常数为表达的上界。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者在结论中报告：所发展的混合时间方法可估计量子态分辨的样本复杂度；对具有均匀先验的任意混合态系综，可用量子弱混合时间给出最小误差分辨样本复杂度的双侧界，并用广义 Dobrushin 系数紧刻画最坏情况分辨样本复杂度；对几何均匀纯态系综，由 Gram 矩阵诱导的 Hadamard 信道的量子齐次混合时间可紧刻画最小误差分辨样本复杂度，且在 Gelfand 对条件下可约化为经典混合问题并用交换 Hecke 代数的表示论量表达；该框架用于恢复量子优惠券收集态的样本复杂度估计，并在特征映射具有三角结构的假设下给出相位态系综混合时间的一般上界，应用于度数为 d 的相位态、广义布尔相位态和超图态。

**创新点**

- 提出一种用于估计量子态分辨样本复杂度的混合时间方法，并给出几何均匀纯态系综最小误差分辨的紧估计。
- 当生成群与稳定子子群构成 Gelfand 对时，将量子混合问题约化为经典混合问题，并用交换 Hecke 代数的表示论量表达广义 Dobrushin 系数。
- 将该框架用于回答若干相位态学习公开问题，并扩展到任意混合态系综、超图态系综以及基于强化数据处理不等式的上界。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

We develop a mixing time method for estimating the sample complexity of quantum state discrimination. We start with considering the minimum-error discrimination of geometrically uniform pure state ensembles, and prove that its sample complexity has a tight estimate given by a quantum homogeneous mixing time [George et al., 2026] and a quantum version of the generalized Dobrushin coefficient [Wolfer, 2020]. This quantum mixing time further reduces to a classical one when the generating group $G$ forms a Gelfand pair with the stabilizer subgroup $H$ of the generator state. In this case the generalized Dobrushin coefficient can be fully expressed by representation-theoretic quantities of the commutative Hecke algebra $\operatorname{End}_G(\mathbb C[G/H])$. In particular, this method reduces the sample complexity estimation of learning quantum coupon collector states [Arunachalam et al., 2020] and learning phase states to classical mixing time problems. We apply this framework to answer the open problems of learning degree-$d$ phase states over $\mathbb F_q$ in [Alrabiah et al., 2026] and generalized Boolean phase states over $\mathbb Z_q$ [Arunachalam et al., 2023]. The framework also applies to hypergraph state ensembles, giving estimates expressed fully in terms of hypergraph data and recovering estimates for graph state ensembles in [Montanaro and Shao, 2022]. Finally, we extend the discussion to arbitrary mixed state ensembles with uniform priors, prove a sandwiched bound for minimum-error discrimination sample complexity by a quantum weakly mixing time, and provide a tight estimate for the minimax discrimination sample complexity from [D'Ariano et al., 2005] by a Dobrushin-type coefficient. We also discuss the method of strengthened data processing inequality [Gao and Rouz{é}, 2022] and give an upper bound in terms of a strengthened data processing inequality constant.

</details>


### [More is Less:Optimal Security for Haar Quantum Money and More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40184)

Zihan Hao、Xingjian Li、Qipeng Liu、Wei Zhan

量子密码 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

量子密码利用不可克隆性来实现经典上不可能的一系列密码应用，例如由量子力学防止伪造的数字货币。安全性要求任何高效用户不能在其已拥有的钞票之外再产生哪怕一张额外有效钞票。然而，现有安全界随着用户可获得的钞票数量增加而减弱。

本文研究一种量子货币构造，只要用户可获得的钞票数量保持低于态层析所需的规模，其渐近查询安全性就不会退化。特别地，我们证明基于 $n$ 量子比特 Haar 随机态和反射 oracle 的构造达到最优查询安全性：除非用户持有 $\Omega(2^n)$ 张钞票，否则其已有钞票不能渐近地加速伪造——其最佳可能攻击与没有任何钞票时相同。为建立这一结果，我们开发了一个基于压缩 oracle 技术的框架，用于定义和分析 Haar 态克隆等量子任务的进展度量。此外，我们证明了生成 $r$ 个额外副本的紧下界，给出了匹配攻击，并将我们的结果应用于量子复制保护。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

摘要称，对于基于 $n$ 量子比特 Haar 随机态和反射 oracle 的量子货币构造，只要用户持有的钞票数低于态层析所需规模，其渐近查询安全性不会随钞票数量增加而退化；除非用户持有 $\Omega(2^n)$ 张钞票，否则已有钞票不能渐近加速伪造，最佳攻击与没有钞票时相同。作者还称建立了基于压缩 oracle 技术的分析框架，证明了生成 $r$ 个额外副本的紧下界并给出匹配攻击，且将结果应用于量子复制保护；这些结论的适用范围限于该构造的渐近查询安全性分析。

**创新点**

- 作者提出基于压缩 oracle 技术的框架，用于定义和分析 Haar 态克隆等量子任务的进展度量。
- 作者证明基于 $n$ 量子比特 Haar 随机态和反射 oracle 的构造达到最优查询安全性，并给出生成 $r$ 个额外副本的紧下界与匹配攻击。
- 作者将上述结果应用于量子复制保护。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Quantum cryptography leverages unclonability to enable a wide range of cryptographic applications that are impossible classically, such as digital currency protected against counterfeiting by quantum mechanics. Security requires that no efficient user can produce even one additional valid banknote beyond those already in their possession. However, existing security bounds weaken as the number of banknotes available to a user increases.
  In this paper, we study a construction of quantum money whose asymptotic query security does not deteriorate as long as the number of banknotes available to a user remains below the scale required for state tomography. In particular, we show that the construction based on an $n$-qubit Haar-random state and a reflection oracle achieves optimal query security: unless a user holds $Ω(2^n)$ banknotes, their existing banknotes cannot asymptotically speed up counterfeiting --- their best possible attack is the same as if they do not have any banknotes. To establish this result, we develop a framework based on the compressed-oracle technique for defining and analyzing progress measures for quantum tasks such as Haar state cloning. Furthermore, we prove a tight lower bound for generating $r$ additional copies, give a matching attack, and apply our results to quantum copy-protection.

</details>


### [The Structure of Higher-Order Quantum Resources with an Application to Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40186)

Felix Ahnefeld、Thomas Theurer、Martin B. Plenio

量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

我们建立了一个高阶量子资源的通用框架，以统一方式处理量子态、信道和超映射（亦称过程）的资源性。通过推广态与信道资源理论中已确立的概念，我们引入并研究了超映射资源单调量以及超映射蒸馏与稀释。作为自由超映射，我们考虑自由网络（可由自由信道实现的超映射）、兼容超映射（在任意复合下封闭的超映射集合）以及最大自由超信道。针对近似信道到信道转换，我们证明在温和假设下，关于菱形距离的转换误差与最坏情况不保真度重合。随后我们将该通用框架应用于动力学相干性资源理论，在其中确立了最大兼容超映射集合的存在性。通过用半正定约束刻画这些集合，我们证明它们严格大于相应的自由网络，但并不包含所有最大自由超信道。最后，我们研究这些集合在单次信道蒸馏与稀释中的操作差异，给出了并行设定下两项任务的解，以及自适应设定下稀释任务的解。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

该工作提出了统一处理量子态、信道与高阶过程资源性的高阶量子资源框架，引入兼容超映射并考察自由网络与最大自由超信道；在近似信道到信道转换中，将相关结果从最坏情况保真度推广到菱形距离，并证明在适当条件下二者对应的一次性可蒸馏资源重合。将框架用于动力学相干性时，作者证明与产生非相干、检测非相干和退相位协变信道兼容的最大超映射集合存在，并用半正定约束刻画，进而表明所考虑的三类自由超映射集合一般不同；在单次信道蒸馏与稀释中，并行与自适应设定下给出解，并显示某些信道上的有限拷贝自适应蒸馏优于并行协议。上述结论主要适用于所考虑的近似转换与动力学相干性资源理论框架，材料未报告实验验证。

**创新点**

- 提出统一处理量子态、信道和超映射资源性的高阶量子资源通用框架。
- 引入兼容超映射概念，并同时考虑自由网络与最大自由超信道。
- 将框架应用于动力学相干性，证明最大兼容超映射集合的存在并用半正定约束刻画，同时给出一击信道蒸馏与稀释在并行及自适应设定下的解。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

We develop a general framework for higher-order quantum resources that treats the resourcefulness of quantum states, channels, and supermaps (also called processes) in a unified manner. By generalizing well-established concepts from resource theories of states and channels, we introduce and study supermap resource monotones and supermap distillation and dilution. As free supermaps, we consider free networks (supermaps that can be implemented with free channels), compatible supermaps (sets of supermaps closed under arbitrary composition), and maximally free superchannels. Specializing to approximate channel-to-channel conversions, we show that under mild assumptions, the conversion error with respect to the diamond distance and the worst-case infidelity coincide. We then apply the general framework to resource theories of dynamical coherence, where we establish the existence of largest sets of compatible supermaps. By characterizing these sets with semidefinite constraints, we show that they are strictly larger than the corresponding free networks but do not contain all maximally free superchannels. We finish by investigating the operational differences of these sets in one-shot channel distillation and dilution, providing solutions for both tasks in the parallel setting and for dilution in the adaptive setting.

</details>


### [Fast classical simulation algorithms for free-fermion dynamics with magic input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40187)

Jiwon Heo、Oliver Reardon-Smith、Michał Oszmaniec、Zoltán Zimborás、Changhun Oh

量子模拟 · 量子算法 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

模拟自由费米子（匹配门）电路的经典成本同时取决于输入态和计算任务。我们针对具有非高斯输入的电路开发了经典算法，处理精确采样以及期望值的加性误差估计和精确估计。对于由 $n$ 份四模魔法态组成的输入，我们给出精确采样算法，将用于玻色采样的 Clifford 和 Clifford 算法推广到被动与主动自由费米子动力学，每样本的最坏情况算术成本为 $O(2^n)$，且没有任何乘性多项式前因子。该被动算法能够对近期一个囚禁离子实验的非相互作用区域进行精确模拟。我们还将最近发展的数量关联函数及相关可观测量的加性误差估计，从被动动力学推广到主动动力学；对于由 $n$ 个四模偶宇称态的乘积形成的输入态，运行时间关于 $n$ 和加性误差的倒数均为多项式。我们的估计器使用适配每个采样态的高斯态分解来控制其二阶矩。这包括对单个输出概率的估计。最后，我们证明：对于由常数大小块组成的偶宇称乘积输入，对数权重的 Majorana 单项式的期望值可以在多项式时间内精确计算，改进了此前具有拟多项式运行时间的 Majorana 传播型算法。该精确算法通过动态规划处理输入块，在 Majorana 因子的子集之间共享计算。我们的算法套件为广泛计算任务范围内的费米子量子模拟提供了经典基准。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者报告了用于模拟具有非高斯块乘积输入的自由费米子电路的经典算法：对 $n$ 份四模魔法态，在被动和主动演化下均可于 $O(2^n)$ 时间内生成精确样本；对任意常数大小偶宇称块，可在多项式时间内精确计算对数权重 Majorana 期望；对一般偶宇称四模块在主动演化下，可在多项式时间内以加性误差估计重叠和数量关联函数。后一结果也覆盖单个输出概率，但本身不能给出完整输出分布的高效采样器，因为逆多项式加性精度不能为指数小概率提供相对误差控制。作者称这些结果为费米子量子实验提供经典基准，并说明可用的模拟保证取决于输入结构、可观测量和所需精度；结论节选还指出这些期望值算法可支持费米子量子机器学习模型的经典训练与评估。

**创新点**

- 针对 $n$ 份四模魔法态输入，给出被动与主动自由费米子动力学下的精确采样算法，每样本最坏情况算术成本为 $O(2^n)$，且无乘性多项式前因子。
- 将数量关联函数及相关可观测量的加性误差估计从被动动力学推广到主动动力学，运行时间关于 $n$ 和加性误差倒数均为多项式。
- 证明由常数大小块组成的偶宇称乘积输入中，对数权重 Majorana 单项式期望可在多项式时间内精确计算，改进此前拟多项式运行时间的 Majorana 传播型算法。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

The classical cost of simulating free-fermion (matchgate) circuits depends on both the input state and the computational task. We develop classical algorithms for circuits with non-Gaussian inputs, addressing exact sampling and both additive-error and exact estimation of expectation values. For inputs consisting of $n$ copies of the four-mode magic state, we give exact sampling algorithms, generalizing the Clifford and Clifford algorithm for Boson sampling, for passive and active free-fermion dynamics with worst-case arithmetic cost $O(2^n)$ per sample, without any multiplicative polynomial prefactor. The passive algorithm enables an exact simulation of the non-interacting regime of a recent trapped-ion experiment. We also extend recently developed additive-error estimation of number correlators and related observables from passive to active dynamics with runtime polynomial in $n$ and the inverse additive-error, for input states formed by products of $n$ four-mode even parity states. Our estimator uses a Gaussian-state decomposition adapted to each sampled state to control its second moment. This includes estimation of individual output probabilities. Finally, we show that for even-parity product inputs composed of constant-size blocks, expectation values of Majorana monomials of logarithmic weight can be computed exactly in polynomial time, improving on prior Majorana propagation-style algorithms with quasi-polynomial runtime. The exact algorithm processes the input blocks by dynamic programming, sharing calculations across subsets of Majorana factors. Our suite of algorithms provide classical benchmarks for fermionic quantum simulations across a broad spectrum of computational tasks.

</details>


### [Query-Limited RAM Programs and their Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40194)

Jiahui Liu、Justin Raizes、Bhaskar Roberts、Omri Shmueli

量子密码

**中文摘要**

量子一次性程序（Broadbent、Gutoski 和 Stebila，CRYPTO 2013），简称 OTP，能够将一项功能编码进一个量子令牌中，该令牌可在单个选定输入上求值，随后变得不可用。尽管这一原语很强大，但它本质上是无状态的，并且与如下设定绑定：每次对电路进行单次求值都需要签发并分发一个量子令牌。这引出一个自然问题：一次性计算范式能否扩展到更丰富、有状态的可控访问形式，并且这种扩展是否会带来超越标准 OTP 的固有优势？
我们引入查询受限 RAM 程序（query-limited RAM programs，QLPs），这是 QOTPs 的 RAM 泛化，支持在有界或策略驱动访问下进行结构化、有状态的计算。QLPs 允许受控的求值序列，同时防止对抗性分叉或计算状态回滚。这带来了超越无状态一次性程序的新应用，包括：其大小仅取决于代码长度（而非运行时间）的图灵机量子令牌；可转移的 k 次或预算受限程序；以及在软件即服务（Software-as-a-Service）等设定中用于委托计算的低通信机制。
为构造 QLPs，我们引入一次性程序（one-shot programs），将一次性签名（Amos、Georgiou、Kiayias 和 Zhandry，STOC 2020）与单有效查询范式（Gupte、Liu、Raizes、Roberts 和 Vaikuntanathan，STOC 2025）统一起来。我们证明一次性程序可通用地蕴含查询受限程序，表明一次性签名更强的不可克隆性保证可转化为增强的功能。在此过程中，我们通过通用构造澄清签名令牌原语与量子一次性程序之间的关系，实质上表明一次性签名程序蕴含一次性通用计算。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

摘要报告的理论结果是：查询受限 RAM 程序（QLPs）可作为量子一次性程序的有状态、受控访问扩展，并可通过一次性程序从一次性签名等原语通用构造得到；其适用范围覆盖有界或策略驱动访问下的受控求值、图灵机量子令牌、可转移 k 次或预算受限程序以及委托计算等理论场景。由于未取得正文结论章节，且摘要未给出实验或具体性能验证，以上结论限于摘要所述的理论构造与蕴含关系。

**创新点**

- 提出查询受限 RAM 程序（QLPs），将一次性计算扩展为有界或策略驱动访问下的有状态、结构化计算。
- 引入一次性程序（one-shot programs），统一一次性签名与单有效查询范式。
- 证明一次性程序可通用蕴含查询受限程序，并澄清签名令牌原语与量子一次性程序的关系。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Quantum one-time programs (Broadbent, Gutoski and Stebila, CRYPTO 2013) or OTPs for short, enable a functionality to be encoded into a quantum token that can be evaluated on a single chosen input and then becomes unusable. While powerful, this primitive is inherently stateless and tied to a setting in which a quantum token needs to be issued and distributed for every single evaluation of a circuit. This raises a natural question: can the one-time computation paradigm be extended to richer, stateful forms of controlled access, and would such an extension offer inherent advantages beyond standard OTPs?
  We introduce $\textit{query-limited RAM programs}$ (QLPs), a RAM-generalization of QOTPs that supports structured, stateful computation under bounded or policy-driven access. QLPs allow controlled sequences of evaluations while preventing adversarial forking or rollback of computational state. This enables new applications beyond stateless one-time programs, including quantum tokens for Turing Machines whose size depends only on $\textit{code length}$ (and not runtime), transferable $k$-time or budget-limited programs, and low-communication mechanisms for delegating computation in settings such as Software-as-a-Service.
  To construct QLPs, we introduce $\textit{one-shot programs}$, unifying one-shot signatures (Amos, Georgiou, Kiayias and Zhandry, STOC 2020) with the single effective query paradigm (Gupte, Liu, Raizes, Roberts and Vaikuntanathan, STOC 2025). We prove that one-shot programs generically imply query-limited programs, demonstrating that the strengthened unclonability guarantees of one-shot signatures translate into enhanced functionality. Along the way, we clarify the relationship between signature-token primitives and quantum one-time programs via generic constructions, essentially showing that one-time signing programs imply one-time general computation.

</details>


### [Generalized Reimpell-Werner Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40203)

Shihao Ru、Bikun Li、Weibo Gao、Liang Jiang

量子测量 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

量子测量和信道决定了量子协议中信息如何被提取、编码和传输。优化它们的性能通常需要随着希尔伯特空间维度增加仍保持实用的数值方法。Reimpell-Werner 迭代通过尊重约束的重复矩阵更新为这些任务提供了一种实用方法。在这里，我们将该迭代推广到具有任意厄米成本矩阵的线性目标。我们证明，只要初始化满足适当的支撑重叠条件，迭代点就会收敛到全局最优。对于每个固定问题、迭代参数选择和允许的初始化，渐近地 $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ 次迭代足以使目标值进入最优值的 $\varepsilon$ 范围内。这些结果为该迭代提供了严格基础，并拓宽了其收敛保证适用的优化问题类别。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者针对在 $\mathrm{tr}_A X\preceq D$ 约束下最大化 $\mathrm{tr}(CX)$ 的正半定矩阵优化问题，提出了广义 Reimpell-Werner 迭代，其中 $C$ 为任意厄米矩阵、$D$ 为正半定矩阵；在给定的移位与支撑条件下，迭代收敛到全局最优，并量化了目标值、证书和矩阵误差的收敛性。文中计时比较显示，在附录 C.1 的计时约定下，原始 RW 与 JRF 实现的中位运行时间明显低于所测试的其他代表性方法。该分析限于当前约束类；推广到一般线性约束需要更新步骤和收敛证明中的新要素，超出本文范围。

**创新点**

- 将 Reimpell-Werner 迭代推广到具有任意厄米成本矩阵的线性目标。
- 在适当的移位与支撑重叠条件下证明迭代收敛到全局最优，并量化目标值、证书和矩阵误差的收敛。
- 对固定问题、迭代参数和可允许初始化给出渐近 $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ 次迭代达到 $\varepsilon$ 目标精度的保证。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Quantum measurements and channels determine how information is extracted, encoded, and transmitted in quantum protocols. Optimizing their performance often requires numerical methods that remain practical as Hilbert space dimensions increase. The Reimpell-Werner iteration offers a practical approach to these tasks through repeated matrix updates that respect the constraints. Here, we generalize this iteration to linear objectives with arbitrary Hermitian cost matrices. We prove that the iterates converge to a global optimum whenever the initialization satisfies suitable support overlap conditions. For each fixed problem, choice of iteration parameters, and admissible initialization, $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ iterations suffice asymptotically to bring the objective value within $\varepsilon$ of the optimum. These results provide a rigorous foundation for the iteration and broaden the class of optimization problems to which its convergence guarantees apply.

</details>


### [Submodularity of entropy under quantum convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40211)

Milad M. Goodarzi

量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

我们为离散量子卷积的冯·诺依曼熵建立了一个次模框架，为熵加性组合学的直接侧提供了非交换对应物。我们首先引入全局加权量子卷积，它们构成以固定输入集合的容许子集为索引的相容族。我们的主定理揭示其熵增长背后的多拟阵几何：相对于任意固定的容许输入块，熵增益可规范化、单调且次模地延拓到其余输入的所有子集。该定理给出卷积强次可加性、量子Ruzsa三角不等式，以及对任意输入态的量子熵Plünnecke--Ruzsa不等式。对于重复输入，它给出跨容许尺度的熵增长的尖锐比较；特别地，量子倍增常数以最优指数控制所有更高阶容许卷积熵。总之，这些结果将加性组合学中的次模方法带入量子情形，并为广泛的卷积熵不等式族提供系统途径。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者在正文中表明，特征核表示可将相关卷积置于共同的熵空间；在输入占据独立寄存器、卷积权重跨子集一致继承、每个容许子集满足所需系数归一化的条件下，普通量子强次可加性可给出物理熵增益相对于每个固定非空容许输入块的规范化、单调、次模延拓。该单一机制统一解释了卷积强次可加性、量子Ruzsa三角不等式和直接Plünnecke–Ruzsa层级。该理论位于熵加性组合学的直接增长侧；从小的量子卷积熵增长中提取类似逆方向结构、并考察其与稳定子结构和magic的关系，被作者列为后续工作。

**创新点**

- 引入全局加权量子卷积，其构成以固定输入集合的容许子集为索引的相容族。
- 证明主定理：相对于任意固定容许输入块，熵增益具有规范化、单调、次模的延拓，并揭示其多拟阵几何。
- 由该框架导出卷积强次可加性、量子Ruzsa三角不等式和量子熵Plünnecke–Ruzsa不等式，并给出重复输入下跨容许尺度的尖锐比较及最优指数控制。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

We develop a submodular framework for the von Neumann entropy of discrete quantum convolutions, providing a noncommutative counterpart to the direct side of entropic additive combinatorics. We first introduce globally weighted quantum convolutions, which form compatible families indexed by admissible subsets of a fixed collection of inputs. Our main theorem reveals a polymatroidal geometry underlying their entropy growth: relative to any fixed admissible input block, the entropy gains admit a normalized, monotone, submodular extension to all subsets of the remaining inputs. The theorem yields convolutional strong subadditivity, quantum Ruzsa triangle inequality, and quantum entropic Plünnecke--Ruzsa inequalities for arbitrary input states. For repeated inputs, it gives sharp comparisons of entropy growth across admissible scales; in particular, the quantum doubling constant controls all higher admissible convolution entropies with optimal exponents. Together, these results bring submodular methods from additive combinatorics into the quantum setting and provide a systematic route to broad families of convolutional entropy inequalities.

</details>


### [Quantum Markov State Models for Metastable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40214)

Hao-En Li、Lin Lin、Michael Ragone

开放量子系统 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

开放量子系统会迅速丢失大部分微观信息，只留下少数自由度支配其长时间动力学。经典马尔可夫状态模型（MSM）将这类亚稳态动力学描述为少数代表性相之间的跃迁，是凝聚态物理和化学物理中成熟的工具。然而，仅凭相标签无法刻画可能在亚稳态之间持续存在的量子相干性。即使慢模已知，其谱投影也未必产生有效的量子态。我们构造量子马尔可夫状态模型（QMSM），在一个包含经典扇区和量子矩阵块的小型物理状态空间上描述存活信息及其演化。我们假设演化信道 $\mathcal C$ 第二次作用时变化很小，其缺陷 $\eta=\|\mathcal C^2-\mathcal C\|_\diamond$ 足够小，并且慢自由度数目的上界与整个系统规模无关。在这些假设下，我们构造压缩与重构信道，其复合可以精确恢复每个约化态，而反向复合给出一个精确幂等的信道来近似 $\mathcal C$，从而回答 Kitaev 的精确舍入问题 \cite{Kitaev2025}。我们的构造对所有输入态给出 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/3})$ 的最优钻石范数界，并对由 $\mathcal C$ 制备的亚稳态给出改进的 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/2})$ 界，其中常数只依赖于慢维度。约化跃迁信道可以迭代使用，以可控误差预测微观动力学。我们用一个弱驱动的耗散自旋链说明 QMSM，该自旋链支持亚稳态逻辑量子比特或长寿命经典相。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

摘要报告：在演化信道二次作用变化很小（缺陷 $\eta$ 足够小）且慢自由度数上界与全系统规模无关的假设下，可构造量子马尔可夫状态模型，通过压缩与重构信道精确恢复约化态，并以精确幂等信道近似原演化信道；所得钻石范数误差界为对所有输入态 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/3})$、对亚稳态 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/2})$，且约化跃迁信道可迭代用于预测微观动力学。上述结论的适用范围受这些假设约束，摘要以弱驱动耗散自旋链作为示例说明。

**创新点**

- 构造量子马尔可夫状态模型，用包含经典扇区和量子矩阵块的小型物理状态空间描述存活信息及其演化。
- 构造压缩与重构信道，使复合能精确恢复每个约化态，反向复合给出精确幂等的信道近似原演化信道，并回答 Kitaev 的精确舍入问题。
- 给出对所有输入态 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/3})$ 的钻石范数界，以及对亚稳态 $\mathcal{O}(\eta^{1/2})$ 的改进界。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Open quantum systems can rapidly lose most microscopic information, leaving only a few degrees of freedom to govern their long-time dynamics. Classical Markov state models (MSMs) describe such metastable dynamics as transitions between a few representative phases and are a well-established tool in condensed matter and chemical physics. However, phase labels alone cannot capture quantum coherence that may persist between metastable states. Even when the slow modes are known, their spectral projection need not produce valid quantum states. We construct quantum Markov state models (QMSMs) that describe the surviving information and its evolution on a small physical state space containing classical sectors and quantum matrix blocks. We assume that the evolution channel $\mathcal C$ changes little when applied a second time, with sufficiently small defect $η=\|\mathcal C^2-\mathcal C\|_\diamond$, and that the number of slow degrees of freedom is bounded independently of the full system size. Under these assumptions, we construct compression and reconstruction channels whose composition recovers every reduced state exactly, while the reverse composition gives an exactly idempotent channel approximating $\mathcal C$, answering Kitaev's exact-rounding question \cite{Kitaev2025}. Our constructions give an optimal diamond-norm bound of $\mathcal{O}(η^{1/3})$ on all input states, as well as an improved bound of $\mathcal{O}(η^{1/2})$ on metastable states prepared by $\mathcal C$, with constants depending only on the slow dimension. The reduced transition channel can be iterated to predict the microscopic dynamics with controlled error. We illustrate the QMSM through a weakly driven dissipative spin chain supporting either a metastable logical qubit or long-lived classical phases.

</details>


### [Universal distillation of quantum entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40215)

Ryuji Takagi、Kaito Watanabe、Takaya Matsuura、Hayato Arai、Masahito Hayashi

量子纠缠 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

可蒸馏纠缠——通过本地操作与经典通信（LOCC）从给定含噪纠缠态中可提取的 Bell 态的最大速率——一直是刻画纠缠态操作价值的核心量。然而，人们已知这一操作意义仅在一个关键假设下成立：双方知道输入态的描述，并能选择为其量身定制的协议；这未必反映自然的操作情境。这里，我们表明即使没有这一假设，可蒸馏纠缠仍是基本的操作量。我们提出一个通用 LOCC 协议——其描述不依赖于输入态，因而对任意输入都普遍适用——该协议能达到任意未知输入态的可蒸馏纠缠。因此，我们的结果将可蒸馏纠缠提升为一个稳健的、具有操作意义的量，并阐明为达到纠缠蒸馏的最优速率并不需要掌握输入态知识。我们的协议基于复合态的状态合并，以及有限块量向可蒸馏纠缠的一致收敛性质。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者表明，在输入态完全未知但以独立同分布（i.i.d.）形式给出的渐近纠缠蒸馏标准设定下，存在一种其描述不依赖于输入态的通用 LOCC 协议，可对任意未知输入态达到其可蒸馏纠缠；因此，输入态知识并非达到最优纠缠蒸馏速率所必需。作者指出该结论不假设输入态具有其他结构（除 i.i.d. 形式外），并认为向非 i.i.d. 输入源、二阶渐近速率及误差衰减最优速率等情形的推广仍有待研究。

**创新点**

- 提出一种与输入态描述无关的通用 LOCC 协议，可对任意未知输入态达到其可蒸馏纠缠。
- 协议基于复合态的状态合并，并结合有限块量向可蒸馏纠缠的一致收敛性质。
- 由此表明可蒸馏纠缠在无需输入态知识的条件下仍是操作上有意义且稳健的量。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Distillable entanglement---the maximum rate of Bell states extractable from the given noisy entangled states via local operations and classical communication (LOCC)---has been a central quantity to characterize the operational value of entangled states. However, this operational meaning had been known to be valid only under a crucial assumption: both parties know the description of the input state and can choose the protocol tailored to it, which does not necessarily reflect the natural operational setting. Here, we show that the distillable entanglement is the fundamental operational quantity even without this assumption. We present a universal LOCC protocol---whose description does not depend on the input state and thus works universally for any input---that achieves the distillable entanglement of an arbitrary unknown input state. Our result therefore lifts the distillable entanglement to a robust notion of an operationally meaningful quantity and clarifies that input-state knowledge is not necessary to achieve the optimal rate of entanglement distillation. Our protocol is based on state merging for compound states, together with the uniform convergence property of the finite-block quantity toward distillable entanglement.

</details>


### [Quantum Log-Determinant Methods for Torsion-Sensitive Topological Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40216)

Dimitrios Thanos、Caesnan Leditto、Adam Wesolowski、Andreea-Iulia Lefterovici、Mahtab Yaghubi Rad

量子算法 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

Betti 数在拓扑数据分析中居核心地位，但无法捕捉整同调中的挠。挠可以区分具有相同 Betti 数的拓扑结构，经典拓扑数据分析中的例子表明它可以影响从数据推断出的拓扑。相关的挠敏感量也已知在基于图的机器学习中具有实际应用，包括生物医学应用。我们研究量子谱方法如何获取这一额外信息。

对数伪行列式通过对非零边界-Laplacian 特征值取对数后求和得到，概括了单纯复形的谱信息。在明确的拓扑假设下，这些量的交错组合给出高阶临界群的大小，即图沙堆群的高维类比。在更强的认证条件下，一种相关构造可恢复同调本身中挠子群的大小。据我们所知，这是首个可证明正确的整同调挠阶量子估计器。

我们给出一种新的量子算法来估计这些量，并显式依赖于输入的查询方式、所需精度和谱隙。平衡多部团复形的精确谱提供了有利的谱隙，一种相关构造给出了显式的带挠基准。借助紧凑查询访问，该算法无需显式构造指数级大的边界矩阵。对于受限的带挠族，在估计对数伪行列式时，定制的振幅估计例程在可比访问条件下使用的查询次数比任何随机经典算法少一个二次因子。将完整的、未归一化的量估计到固定加性误差，其成本仍与候选空间维度 $D$ 成正比。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

该工作表明，非零边界谱也是可算法访问的算术信息来源；量子步骤无需单独的秩输入即可估计归一化边界对数伪行列式，而矩阵-树恒等式给出这些谱量获得挠敏感解释的条件。作者指出，仅有大的链空间通常不足以带来有意义的量子节省，效率与解释保证还取决于相干访问、谱隙、精度要求以及相应的拓扑证书；其示例表明这些要求可与非平凡挠相容，并可通过避免显式构造边界矩阵以及在受控 oracle 设置中获得量子查询优势来节省成本。将框架扩展到结构更少和由数据导出的复形、匹配的经典比较以及跨滤波的稳健行为，是迈向挠敏感量子拓扑数据分析的下一步。

**创新点**

- 提出一种估计边界对数伪行列式等谱量的量子算法，其复杂度显式依赖于查询方式、所需精度和谱隙。
- 作者称这是首个可证明正确的整同调挠阶量子估计器，并在更强认证下恢复同调中挠子群的大小。
- 针对受限带挠族，定制振幅估计在可比访问下比随机经典算法少一个二次因子的查询，并避免显式构造指数级边界矩阵。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Betti numbers are central to topological data analysis but fail to capture torsion in integral homology. Torsion can distinguish topological structures with identical Betti numbers, and examples from classical topological data analysis show that it can affect the topology inferred from data. Related torsion-sensitive quantities are also known to have practical applications in graph-based machine learning, including biomedical applications. We investigate how quantum spectral methods can access this additional information.
  Log pseudodeterminants, obtained by summing the logarithms of the nonzero boundary-Laplacian eigenvalues, summarize spectral information about a simplicial complex. Under explicit topological assumptions, an alternating combination of these quantities gives the size of a higher critical group, the higher-dimensional analogue of a graph sandpile group. With stronger certification, a related construction recovers the size of the torsion subgroup in homology itself. This is, to our knowledge, the first provably correct quantum estimator of integral-homology torsion order.
  We give a new quantum algorithm for estimating these quantities, with explicit dependence on how the input is queried, the required accuracy, and the spectral gaps. Exact spectra for balanced multipartite clique complexes provide favorable gaps, and a related construction gives an explicit torsion-bearing benchmark. With compact query access, the algorithm need not construct the exponentially large boundary matrices explicitly. For a restricted torsion-bearing family, a tailored amplitude-estimation routine uses quadratically fewer queries than any randomized classical algorithm given comparable access when estimating the log pseudodeterminant. Estimating the full, unnormalized quantity to fixed additive error still costs in proportion to the candidate-space dimension $D$.

</details>


### [Improved quantum volume estimation with transducers and amortized quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40217)

Arjan Cornelissen、Simon Apers、Sander Gribling

量子算法 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

体积估计问题是计算几何中的一个经典任务。针对该问题的随机算法的发展，推动了许多与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛和模拟退火相关的重要算法技术的发展，并且该问题与若干重要几何结果相联系，例如最近得到解决的 KLS 猜想。在本工作中，我们将 Cousins 和 Vempala 提出的最先进的 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$ 查询随机算法量子化，得到一个 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{1.75}/\varepsilon)$ 查询的量子算法，改进了当前最优的 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{2.25}/\varepsilon)$ 界。我们的关键技术创新是一个用于摊销量子行走代价的框架。该框架基于 Belovs、Jeffery 和 Yolcu 最近引入的 transducer 工具包。正是这种摊销量子行走框架使我们能够利用 Cousins 和 Vempala 对球行走的摊销分析，从而克服此前阻碍其量子实现的关键障碍。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

仅依据摘要，作者将 Cousins 和 Vempala 的体积估计随机算法量子化，把查询复杂度从 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{2.25}/\varepsilon)$ 改进到 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{1.75}/\varepsilon)$；其关键做法是借助基于 transducer 工具包的摊销量子行走框架，利用球行走的摊销分析来克服此前的量子实现障碍。未取得正文结论章节，因此材料未进一步说明更广泛的适用范围或独立验证情况。

**创新点**

- 将 Cousins 和 Vempala 的 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$ 查询随机体积估计算法量子化，得到 $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{1.75}/\varepsilon)$ 查询的量子算法。
- 提出一个基于 Belovs、Jeffery 和 Yolcu 的 transducer 工具包的摊销量子行走框架。
- 利用该摊销量子行走框架来利用 Cousins 和 Vempala 对球行走的摊销分析，从而克服此前阻碍其量子实现的关键障碍。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

The volume estimation problem is a classic task in computational geometry. The development of randomized algorithms for this problem spurred the development of many influential algorithmic techniques related to Markov Chain Monte Carlo and simulated annealing, and the problem connects to several important geometrical results, like the recently-resolved KLS conjecture. In this work, we quantize the state-of-the-art $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$-query randomized algorithm developed by Cousins and Vempala, and obtain a $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{1.75}/\varepsilon)$-query quantum algorithm, improving over the $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{2.25}/\varepsilon)$ state-of-the-art bound. Our key technical contribution is a framework for amortizing the cost of a quantum walk. The framework is based on the recent transducer toolkit introduced by Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu. It is this amortized quantum walk framework that allows us to exploit the amortized analysis of the ball walk by Cousins and Vempala, thus overcoming the key barrier that previously barred its quantum implementation.

</details>


### [Purification brings advantages in sequential quantum channel discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40218)

Zoe G. del Toro、Marco Túlio Quintino、Jessica Bavaresco

量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

纯化通常被用作量子态和信道的便捷数学表示，尤其是在构造量子信息处理任务协议时。尽管这一视角很有力，但它可能暗示纯化自由度及其与环境的关联只是有用的表示变换。这里我们展示，在顺序量子信道分辨中，它们反而可以被当作一种操作资源来利用，即使环境参考系未知或被平均化。与态分辨和并行信道分辨（对二者而言，裸访问和平均化纯化访问是等价的）不同，对纯化资源的顺序访问在成功概率上表现出严格优势。我们通过两个主要结果证明这种严格分离。第一个结果中，我们构造了两族 qubit-qubit 信道，在两次查询情形下具有严格分离；第二个结果中，我们给出了一对 qubit-qubit 信道的例子，其纯化在三次查询下可完美分辨，而任意有限次裸（非纯化）查询都不足以实现完美分辨。这些分离意味着在信道分辨中顺序策略相对于并行策略的优势，并确立了如下不可能性：通过允许查询之间任意干预的变换，将裸信道查询转化为平均化纯化查询是不可能的，这加强了先前的结果。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

在裸信道查询、平均化纯化查询以及固定但未知纯化查询三种访问模型下，作者发现对于量子到经典任务，两种纯化访问模型在并行和顺序策略下均操作等价；在并行设置中，两种纯化访问都不比裸查询有优势。顺序策略下该等价性被打破：所构造的一对 qubit-qubit 信道族在两次纯化查询下比两次裸查询具有严格更高的分辨概率，另一对信道族可用三次纯化查询完美分辨，而任意有限次裸查询都不能完美分辨。由此，作者得出相应资源中顺序策略优于并行策略，并给出裸查询一般不能经允许任意中间干预的变换转化为平均化纯化查询的操作性证明。结论还指出，共同环境幺正使连续查询的纯化输出相关联，Haar 平均将这些关联组织成不变置换扇区而非破坏它们，顺序协议可保留早期环境信息并与后续输出结合；这些结果针对所构造的信道族与分辨任务，说明纯化关联可产生可观测的操作后果。

**创新点**

- 提出纯化在顺序量子信道分辨中可作为操作资源，即使环境参考系未知或被平均化。
- 构造两查询下具有严格成功概率分离的 qubit-qubit 信道族，并构造三查询纯化可完美分辨、任意有限裸查询均不能完美分辨的例子。
- 给出裸信道查询一般不能通过允许查询间任意干预的变换转化为平均化纯化查询的操作性证明。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Purifications are often used as a convenient mathematical representation of quantum states and channels when constructing protocols for quantum information processing tasks. Although powerful, this perspective can suggest that the purifying degrees of freedom and its correlations with an environment are just a useful change of representation. Here we show how they can instead be exploited as an operational resource in sequential quantum channel discrimination, even when the environmental reference frame is unknown or averaged. In contrast to state discrimination and parallel channel discrimination, for which bare and averaged-purification access are equivalent, sequential access to purified resources exhibits a strict advantage in probability of success. We prove this strict separation via two main results. In the first, we construct a pair of families of qubit-qubit channels with a strict separation in the two-query case, and in the second, we show an example of a pair of qubit-qubit channels whose purifications are perfectly distinguishable with three queries while no finite number of bare (non-purified) queries suffices for perfect discrimination. These separations imply an advantage of sequential over parallel strategies for channel discrimination and establish the impossibility of converting bare channel queries into averaged-purification queries via transformations that allow arbitrary interventions between queries, strengthening previous results.

</details>


### [NLTM Hamiltonians from gauged sheaf quantum locally testable codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40220)

Fuchuan Wei、Zhengyi Han、Zimu Li、Zi-Wen Liu

量子复杂性 · 量子纠错 · 量子多体物理

**中文摘要**

理解低能量子态的复杂性是量子复杂性理论和多体物理的核心问题。本文证明无低能平凡魔性（no low-energy trivial magic, NLTM）猜想，构造一族局域哈密顿量，使得在正能量密度窗口内的每个态都需要电路深度 Ω(log n) 才能从任意稳定子态制备，而这些稳定子态本身可能高度纠缠。值得注意的是，我们的结果具有内禀性：同一哈密顿量族和能量密度阈值适用于任意具有任意有界局域维度的 qudit 稳定子输入。我们的构造建立在非阿贝尔规范层码之上，这些码利用好的量子局部可测试码上的杯积结构获得。我们为相关哈密顿量建立了常数算子可靠性，使其受保护的逻辑结构能够约束足够低能量密度下的所有态。通过排除浅层基于稳定子的经典见证，我们的结果显著加强了 NLTS，并推进了我们对与量子 PCP 相关的低能复杂性的理解。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者利用层 qLDPC 码的最新进展建立了内禀 NLTM 概念，扩展了 NLTS 通向 qPCP 所排除的经典低能见证的范围，并把量子纠错与稳健量子电路复杂性之间的联系推广到稳定子码之外。其关键论点是：非阿贝尔规范码基态简并度的算术性质可阻碍从任意稳定子态进行浅层制备，而线性距离和常数可靠性把这种阻碍延伸到线性能量窗口；作者通过非阿贝尔规范层码构造非加性量子局部可测试码来实现这一点。该结论适用于所构造的局域哈密顿量族及有界局域维度的 qudit 稳定子输入，并表明量子纠错可作为在非零温度下持续存在的稳健低能性质来保护超越纠缠的复杂性。

**创新点**

- 作者证明 NLTM 猜想，并构造局域哈密顿量族，使正能量密度窗口内的任一态从任意稳定子态制备都需要 Ω(log n) 电路深度。
- 作者给出内禀结果：同一哈密顿量族和能量密度阈值适用于任意有界局域维度的 qudit 稳定子输入。
- 作者利用好的量子局部可测试码上的杯积结构构造非阿贝尔规范层码，并建立相关哈密顿量的常数算子可靠性。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Understanding the complexity of low-energy quantum states is a central problem in quantum complexity theory and many-body physics. Here we prove the no low-energy trivial magic (NLTM) conjecture, constructing a family of local Hamiltonians for which every state within a positive energy density window requires circuit depth $Ω(\log n)$ to prepare from arbitrary stabilizer state that may themselves be highly entangled. Notably, our result is intrinsic in the sense that the same Hamiltonian family and energy density threshold apply to arbitrary qudit stabilizer inputs with any bounded local dimensions. Our construction builds on non-Abelian gauged sheaf codes obtained using the cup product structure on good quantum locally testable codes. We establish constant operator soundness for the associated Hamiltonians, enabling their protected logical structure to constrain all states at sufficiently low energy density. By excluding shallow stabilizer-based classical witnesses, Our result substantially strengthens NLTS and advances our understanding of low-energy complexity relevant to quantum PCP.

</details>


### [Causal inequalities witness non-stabilizerness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40223)

Leonardo Vaglini、Nasra Daher Ahmed、Ravi Kunjwal

量子信息理论 · 量子基础

**中文摘要**

稳定子操作描述了量子理论中一个已知可由经典计算机高效模拟的片段，这归功于Gottesman-Knill定理。因此，诸如魔法态之类的非稳定子资源对于通用量子计算是必要的。有趣的是，魔法资源理论的两种进路——操作式进路与公理化进路——并不相同：前者的自由操作集合，即稳定子操作（SO），严格小于后者的自由操作集合，即完全保持稳定子操作（CSPO）。一个展示这种分离的简单例子由一个三量子比特稳定子乘积基给出：其态不能使用SO被完美区分，但确实允许使用CSPO进行完美区分。这样的态集合被称为表现出无魔法非稳定子性（NSWM）。在这里，我们获得对这一现象的原则性理解，证明其存在的充要条件。我们首先推导出一个简单判据，用来判定给定稳定子基的态能否仅使用稳定子操作被完美区分。然后我们考虑稳定子基只包含乘积态的情形，并利用其与过程函数——无悖论因果环的经典模型——的联系来证明以下结论：稳定子乘积基中的态需要非稳定子性才能被完美区分，当且仅当对应的过程函数违反因果不等式。这为因果不等式违反作为非稳定子性的见证提供了一种新的操作意义，而非稳定子性是计算非经典性的一种形式。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

该文在魔法资源的操作式框架内研究了稳定子态正交完备集的态区分问题：定理1给出判定任意稳定子基（包括含纠缠态者）能否仅用稳定子操作完美区分的递归判据；对于稳定子乘积基，定理2表明其能否用SO完美区分等价于对应过程函数是否因果，因而NSWM当且仅当该过程函数违反因果不等式。作者据此把因果不等式违反解释为非稳定子性的见证，并将相关结论推广到任意素数维度；LOCC与SO在近似区分成功率上是否存在差距仍为开放问题。

**创新点**

- 提出一个简单的递归判据（定理1），用于判定任意稳定子基——包括含纠缠态的稳定子基——能否仅用稳定子操作完美区分。
- 证明稳定子乘积基需要非稳定子性才能被完美区分当且仅当其对应过程函数违反因果不等式（定理2），从而把因果不等式违反作为非稳定子性的见证。
- 将此前关于无魔法非稳定子性（NSWM）的观察推广到任意素数维度，并给出NSWM的充要刻画。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Stabilizer operations describe a fragment of quantum theory that is known to be efficiently classically simulable, thanks to the Gottesman-Knill theorem. For this reason, nonstabilizer resources such as magic states are necessary for universal quantum computation. Interestingly, the operational and axiomatic approaches to the resource theory of magic differ: the set of free operations in the former, namely, stabilizer operations (SO), is strictly smaller than that in the latter, namely, completely stabilizer preserving operations (CSPO). A simple example showing the separation is given by a three-qubit stabilizer product basis whose states cannot be perfectly discriminated using SO, but which do admit perfect discrimination using CSPO. Such an ensemble of states is said to exhibit nonstabilizerness without magic (NSWM). Here we obtain a principled understanding of this phenomenon, proving necessary and sufficient conditions for its existence. We first derive a simple criterion to decide whether, given a stabilizer basis, its states can be perfectly discriminated using stabilizer operations alone. We then consider the case where the stabilizer basis contains only product states and use its link with process functions---classical models of paradox-free causal loops---to prove the following: the states in a stabilizer product basis require nonstabilizerness for perfect discrimination if and only if the corresponding process function violates a causal inequality. This provides a new operational meaning to causal inequality violations as witnesses of nonstabilizerness, a form of computational nonclassicality.

</details>


### [A physical and universal model of bosonic computations with Solovay-Kitaev theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40226)

Dorian Rudolph、Arsalan Motamedi、Dhruva Sambrani、Hamid Reza Naeij、Ulysse Chabaud、Sevag Gharibian、Saeed Mehraban

量子算法 · 量子复杂性 · 光量子技术

**中文摘要**

玻色量子系统是量子信息处理的领先架构之一，具有带强纠错能力的连续变量自由度。然而，标准玻色量子计算模型，例如 Lloyd-Braunstein [Lloyd and Braunstein, 1999] 和混合振子-量子比特模型 [Brenner, Dias, and Koenig, 2025; Liu et al., 2026]，允许能量剧烈增长，导致非物理的计算能力，并使通用且高效编译等基本算法工具失效 [Brenner et al., 2026; Rudolph et al., 2025]。为解决这一问题，我们引入一种新的玻色量子计算模型——玻色能量守恒量子计算（Bosonic Energy-Preserving Quantum Computation, BEQC），其中能量被视为一种计算资源。也就是说，能量仅通过输入相干态提供，所有门均由能量守恒哈密顿量生成。因此，按构造，能量剧烈增长是不可能的，使该模型具有物理基础。

我们接下来证明，BEQC 在许多方面是计算稳健且通用的模型，包括：（1. 计算能力）BEQC 高效模拟现有模型中的所有多项式能量计算，并在多项式能量设定下精确恢复 BQP。当改变模型的能量、精度和空间参数时，它进一步容许若干复杂度理论上的上界。（2. 通用门集与态合成）BEQC 具有基于线性光学和 Kerr 相互作用的自然通用门集。特别地，我们得到一个 Solovay-Kitaev 定理，它规避了先前的 no-go 结果。我们给出多种应用，包括（a）具有严格制备保证的 GKP 态工程协议，（b）以指数精度制备 Fock 态，以及（c）对任意基于量子比特的酉操作进行原生 Fock 空间模拟。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

摘要提出 BEQC 模型：能量仅通过输入相干态提供，所有门由能量守恒哈密顿量生成，从而按构造排除能量剧烈增长；摘要并声称该模型在多项式能量设定下精确恢复 BQP、能高效模拟现有模型中的多项式能量计算，且具有基于线性光学与 Kerr 相互作用的通用门集和规避先前 no-go 结果的 Solovay-Kitaev 定理。上述结论限于摘要所述模型与参数设定，且未提供正文结论章节，不能视为独立验证。

**创新点**

- 作者提出 BEQC 模型，将能量作为计算资源，能量仅由输入相干态提供，所有门由能量守恒哈密顿量生成，从构造上排除能量剧烈增长。
- 作者声称 BEQC 高效模拟现有模型中的多项式能量计算，并在多项式能量设定下精确恢复 BQP，同时给出随能量、精度和空间参数变化的复杂度上界。
- 作者给出基于线性光学与 Kerr 相互作用的自然通用门集及规避先前 no-go 结果的 Solovay-Kitaev 定理，并应用于 GKP 态、Fock 态制备和任意量子比特酉的原生 Fock 空间模拟。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Bosonic quantum systems are among the leading architectures for quantum information processing, offering continuous-variable degrees of freedom with strong error-correction capabilities. However, standard bosonic quantum computation models such as the Lloyd-Braunstein [Lloyd and Braunstein, 1999] and hybrid oscillator-qubit models [Brenner, Dias, and Koenig, 2025; Liu et al., 2026] permit dramatic energy growth, leading to unphysical computational power and the breakdown of fundamental algorithmic tools such as universal and efficient compilation [Brenner et al., 2026; Rudolph et al., 2025]. To address this, we introduce a new model of bosonic quantum computation, Bosonic Energy-Preserving Quantum Computation (BEQC), in which energy is treated as a computational resource. Namely, energy is supplied solely through input coherent states, and all gates are generated by energy-preserving Hamiltonians. Thus, by construction, dramatic energy growth is impossible, making the model physically grounded.
  We next show that BEQC is a computationally robust and universal model in many respects, including: (1. Computational power) BEQC efficiently simulates all polynomial-energy computations in existing models, and exactly recovers BQP in the polynomial energy setting. It further admits several complexity-theoretic upper bounds when varying the energy, precision, and space parameters of the model. (2. Universal gate sets and state synthesis) BEQC has natural universal gate sets based on linear optics and Kerr interactions. In particular, we obtain a Solovay-Kitaev theorem which circumvents previous no-go results. We give various applications, including (a) a protocol for engineering GKP states with rigorous preparation guarantees, (b) Fock state preparation to exponential precision, and (c) native Fock space simulation of any qubit-based unitary.

</details>


### [Randomness of exact unitary designs under symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40227)

Christopher Vairogs、Felix Leditzky

量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

我们将有限酉系综的酉设计强度定义为使其第$t$阶统计矩与酉群相同的最大的整数$t$。在存在物理对称性时，并非系综中的所有算子都与对称性兼容。这促使我们将有限酉系综的对称设计强度定义为：使其与对称性兼容的子系综的第$t$阶矩与对称兼容酉群的第$t$阶矩相匹配的最大整数$t$。我们证明，在足够高的维数下，对于广泛的对称性族（包括全局在位 $\mathrm{U}(1)$ 和 $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ 对称性），存在具有任意高设计强度的有限酉系综，其对称设计强度严格更高。对于依赖酉随机化的协议，具有该性质的系综在对称性约束下可能比在没有对称性时更具通用性。我们的结果推广了先前的工作 [Mitsuhashi and Yoshioka, PRX Quantum 4.4 (Nov. 2023)]：该工作表明，对于不会使对称兼容酉算子平凡化的对称性，Clifford群的对称设计强度严格地以其酉设计强度为上界。由于具有群结构的酉设计具有特殊意义，我们还导出了算子构成群的有限酉系综的酉设计强度和对称设计强度的多种界。特别是，我们给出了一个简单证明：在全局在位 $\mathrm{U}(1)$ 和 $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ 对称性下，任意由算子构成有限群的系综，其对称设计强度至多为二。在此过程中，我们证明在任意维数下存在具有任意高对称设计强度的均匀加权群，并分析了它们的结构。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者构造了有限酉系综，并证明在一类满足技术表示论条件的广泛对称性下，其对称设计强度可严格高于酉设计强度；该结果适用于足够高维及所涵盖的对称族，尚不能覆盖所有闭对称群。作者还给出算子成群的有限系综的设计强度界，证明在全局在位 $\mathrm{U}(1)$ 和 $\mathrm{SU}(d)$（结论节选中具体提到 $\mathrm{SU}(2)$）对称性下任意有限群系综的对称设计强度至多二，并证明任意维存在任意高对称设计强度的均匀加权群。作者指出，所构造的对称提升设计尚未以具体局域量子门给出编译，因而其用于带对称约束的随机化协议仍需门编译。

**创新点**

- 定义了有限酉系综的对称设计强度，用于刻画与对称性兼容子系综的矩匹配阶数。
- 证明在足够高维和一类广泛对称性下，存在设计强度任意高且对称设计强度严格更高的有限酉系综。
- 给出算子成群的有限酉系综的对称设计强度界，并构造和分析任意维中具有任意高对称设计强度的均匀加权群。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

We define the unitary design strength of a finite unitary ensemble to be the maximal integer $t$ for which its $t$-th statistical moment is identical to that of the unitary group. In the presence of physical symmetry, not all operators of an ensemble are symmetry-compatible. This motivates us to define the symmetric design strength of a finite unitary ensemble to be the maximal integer $t$ for which the $t$-th moments of its symmetry-compatible sub-ensemble match those of the group of symmetry-compatible unitaries. We show that in sufficiently high dimension and for a broad family of symmetries, which includes the global on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ symmetries, there exist finite unitary ensembles of arbitrarily high design strength whose symmetric design strength is strictly higher. Ensembles with this property may be more versatile under symmetry constraints for protocols that rely on unitary randomization than they are without symmetry. Our results extend the previous work [Mitsuhashi and Yoshioka, PRX Quantum 4.4 (Nov. 2023)] showing that the symmetric design strength of the Clifford group is strictly upper bounded by its unitary design strength for symmetries that do not trivialize the symmetry-compatible unitaries. Due to the special significance of unitary designs with a group structure, we also derive a variety of bounds on the unitary and symmetric design strengths of finite unitary ensembles whose operators form a group. In particular, we provide a simple proof that an arbitrary ensemble of operators forming a finite group must have a symmetric design strength of at most two under the global on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ symmetries. Along the way, we show that there exist uniformly weighted groups of arbitrarily high symmetric design strength in arbitrary dimension and analyze their structure.

</details>


### [Perfect non-local quantum computation is impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40228)

Marten Folkertsma、Dmitry Grinko、Gina Muuss、Florian Speelman

量子信息理论 · 量子纠缠 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

非局域量子计算（NLQC）要求两方利用一个纠缠资源态和一轮同时量子通信，对各自的量子输入施加一个联合操作。NLQC 在量子信息中有多种应用，包括对量子位置验证的攻击、通信复杂性以及量子引力。

每个二体酉操作都有一个使用有限纠缠的近似 NLQC 协议，并且若干结构化族已知存在精确协议。然而，是否每个固定酉操作都容许一个使用有限维资源的精确协议，此前仍未解决。我们证明，即使对于两量子比特，Haar 几乎每一个酉操作也不容许这样的协议，即使资源态和局部操作是针对目标定制的。

证明首先表明，对精确协议作光滑形变只能通过局部酉操作改变其目标。对于固定架构，半代数几何把精确协议限制为有限多个连通族，因此每个架构只能达到有限多个局部酉轨道。对所有架构取并，可精确实现的酉操作构成可数多个局部酉轨道。我们还表明，一些自然目标不容许协议：受控相位 $\mathrm{diag}(1,1,1,e^{i\theta})$ 在 $e^{i\theta}$ 为超越数时没有使用有限维资源的精确协议，例如当 $\theta=1$ 时。

我们的结果也适用于可局域化量子测量：其结果通过在不通信的情况下对输入和共享纠缠资源态进行局部测量获得。在 Haar 随机基下的秩一投影测量几乎必然不能用一个有限维资源态和有限多个局部结果实现局域化。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者证明：在有限维资源下可精确实现的酉操作集合是局部酉轨道的可数并，因而 Haar 测度为零；因此，即使对于两量子比特，Haar 几乎每一个酉操作也不存在精确 NLQC 协议，而且即使资源态和局部操作按目标定制也无法消除这种不可能性。对可局域化量子测量，作者也给出类似的测度为零结论。适用范围为精确协议与有限维资源。

**创新点**

- 作者证明即使两量子比特，Haar 几乎每一个酉操作也没有使用有限维资源的精确 NLQC 协议，即使资源态和局部操作按目标定制。
- 作者用光滑形变、半代数几何和局部酉轨道论证，将可精确实现的酉操作限制为可数多个局部酉轨道，从而使其 Haar 测度为零。
- 作者指出受控相位 $\mathrm{diag}(1,1,1,e^{i\theta})$ 在 $e^{i\theta}$ 为超越数（如 $\theta=1$）时无精确有限维协议，并证明 Haar 随机基下的秩一投影测量几乎必然不可局域化。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) asks two parties to apply a joint operation to their quantum inputs using an entangled resource state and one round of simultaneous quantum communication. NLQC has applications across quantum information, including attacks on quantum position verification, communication complexity, and quantum gravity.
  Every bipartite unitary has an approximate NLQC protocol with finite entanglement, and exact protocols are known for several structured families. However, it has remained open whether every fixed unitary admits an exact protocol with finite-dimensional resources. We show that, even for two qubits, Haar-almost every unitary does not admit such a protocol, even when the resource state and local operations are tailored to the target.
  The proof first shows that smooth deformations of an exact protocol can only change its target by local unitaries. For a fixed architecture, semialgebraic geometry limits the exact protocols to finitely many connected families, so each architecture reaches only finitely many local-unitary orbits. Taking the union over all architectures, the exactly implementable unitaries form countably many local-unitary orbits. We also show that some natural targets do not admit a protocol: the controlled phase $\mathrm{diag}(1,1,1,e^{iθ})$ has no exact protocol with finite-dimensional resources whenever $e^{iθ}$ is transcendental, for instance for $θ= 1$.
  Our results also apply to localizable quantum measurements, whose outcome is obtained from local measurements on inputs and a shared entangled resource state without communication. A rank-one projective measurement in a Haar-random basis is almost surely not localizable with a finite-dimensional resource state and finitely many local outcomes.

</details>


### [A Quantum Algorithm for $st$-Transport on Flat Connection Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40231)

Stacey Jeffery、Tobias J. Osborne、Galina Pass

量子算法 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

我们研究无向 $st$-连通性到边携带量子操作的图的一种推广。设 $G=(V,E)$ 是 $n$ 个顶点上的无向图，其中每条边 $\{u,v\}$ 被标记为酉算子 $U_{uv}\in\mathbb{C}^{k\times k}$，且 $U_{vu}=U_{uv}^\dagger$。我们假设这些标记构成一个\emph{平坦}连接：任意一对顶点 $u$ 和 $v$ 之间沿任意路径的标记有序乘积与路径无关。等价地，该连接是纯规范，即规范等价于平凡连接；这类图正是谱图论中的一致连接图以及群同步的无噪声实例。因此，只要 $s$ 和 $t$ 连通，从 $s$ 到 $t$ 传输一个态就定义一个唯一酉算子 $U_s(t)$。给定态 $|\psi_s\rangle,|\psi_t\rangle\in\mathbb{C}^k$ 以及一个在相干地应用相应边酉算子的同时返回某顶点邻居的 oracle，\emph{$st$-transport 问题}是判定 $s$ 和 $t$ 是否连通，并在连通时估计 $U_s(t)|\psi_s\rangle$ 与 $|\psi_t\rangle$ 之间平方重叠到加性误差 $\varepsilon$。当 $k=1$ 且所有标记为平凡时，这恰好就是无向 $st$-连通性。我们给出一个用于 $st$-transport 的有界误差量子算法，运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$，使用 $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 空间。我们通过设计一个 transducer 并对输入图应用 Metropolis-Hastings 重加权来实现这一点。我们还证明一个 $\Omega(n)$ 量子查询下界，即使承诺 $s$ 和 $t$ 连通时该下界仍成立，因此对于常数 $\varepsilon$，我们的算法在对数多项式因子内是最优的。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

现有材料未提供正文结论章节，仅摘要表明：在有界误差下，所给量子算法可在时间 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ 和使用 $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 空间内求解平坦连接图上的 $st$-transport；摘要还给出即使 $s$ 与 $t$ 承诺连通仍成立的 $\Omega(n)$ 量子查询下界，因此在常数 $\varepsilon$ 下该算法在对数多项式因子内最优。上述结论的适用范围限于摘要所描述的平坦连接图与相应 oracle 模型。

**创新点**

- 提出一种有界误差量子算法，在 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ 时间和 $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 空间内求解平坦连接图上的 $st$-transport。
- 通过设计 transducer 并对输入图应用 Metropolis-Hastings 重加权来实现该算法。
- 证明即使 $s$ 和 $t$ 承诺连通仍成立的 $\Omega(n)$ 量子查询下界，并由此说明常数 $\varepsilon$ 下算法在对数多项式因子内最优。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

We study a generalization of undirected $st$-connectivity to graphs whose edges carry quantum operations. Let $G=(V,E)$ be an undirected graph on $n$ vertices in which each edge $\{u,v\}$ is labeled by a unitary $U_{uv}\in\mathbb{C}^{k\times k}$, with $U_{vu}=U_{uv}^\dagger$. We assume the labels form a \emph{flat} connection: the ordered product of labels along any path between a pair of vertices $u$ and $v$ is independent of the path. Equivalently, the connection is pure gauge, i.e., gauge-equivalent to the trivial connection; such graphs are exactly the consistent connection graphs of spectral graph theory and the noiseless instances of group synchronization. Consequently, whenever $s$ and $t$ are connected, transporting a state from $s$ to $t$ defines a unique unitary $U_s(t)$. Given states $|ψ_s\rangle,|ψ_t\rangle\in\mathbb{C}^k$ and an oracle that returns the neighbours of a vertex while coherently applying the corresponding edge unitaries, the \emph{$st$-transport problem} is to decide whether $s$ and $t$ are connected and, if so, to estimate the squared overlap between $U_s(t)|ψ_s\rangle$ and $|ψ_t\rangle$ to additive error $\varepsilon$. When $k=1$ and all labels are trivial, this is exactly undirected $st$-connectivity. We give a bounded-error quantum algorithm for $st$-transport that runs in time $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ and uses $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ space. We do this by designing a transducer and applying a Metropolis-Hastings reweighting to the input graph. We also prove an $Ω(n)$ quantum query lower bound that holds even when $s$ and $t$ are promised to be connected, so for constant $\varepsilon$ our algorithm is optimal up to polylogarithmic factors.

</details>


### [Optimal Purity Estimation with Incoherent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40248)

Junseo Lee、Chirag Wadhwa

量子信息理论 · 量子测量 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

在这项工作中，我们考虑一个基本任务：在乘性误差 ε 内估计未知态的纯度。当可以进行一般的集体测量时，已知该问题需要且仅需要 Θ\left(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d}{\varepsilon}\right) 个副本 [AISW20]。然而，在如此多的副本上实现集体测量在实验上可能要求很高，因此我们旨在刻画使用非相干测量时该问题的副本复杂度。

在此设置下，唯一非平凡的结果是一个使用 O\left(\frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon}\right) 个副本的非自适应算法 [PTTW26]，它比已知最佳下界多项式级地更大。我们的第一个结果是一个执行非自适应非相干测量的新算法，它使用 O\left(\frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}\right) 个副本即可成功，从而改进了副本复杂度中的后一项。此外，我们证明对于任何限于非自适应测量的算法，上述副本复杂度是最优的。

我们还开发了一种新的态纯度自适应估计器，在高精度情形，即 ε = o(1/d) 时，它改进了上述复杂度。我们还证明，非自适应与自适应估计器共同给出了非相干纯度估计的最优复杂度；特别地，我们证明该问题的副本复杂度为 Θ\left(\min\left\{\frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}, \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon} + \frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2}\right\}\right)。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

仅依据摘要，作者报告：在非相干测量下，纯度乘性误差估计可由使用 O\left(\frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}\right) 个副本的非自适应算法实现，且该复杂度在非自适应测量类中最优；结合在高精度区 ε = o(1/d) 改进复杂度的自适应估计器，非相干纯度估计的副本复杂度为 Θ\left(\min\left\{\frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}, \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon} + \frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2}\right\}\right)。现有材料未提供正文结论章节，未说明该结论在所述设定之外的适用范围。

**创新点**

- 提出使用 O\left(\frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}\right) 个副本的非自适应非相干测量算法，改进了已有副本复杂度中的后一项。
- 证明在仅限非自适应测量的算法中，上述副本复杂度最优。
- 提出在高精度区 ε = o(1/d) 改进复杂度的自适应纯度估计器，并与非自适应估计器共同给出非相干纯度估计的最优副本复杂度。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

In this work, we consider the fundamental task of estimating the purity of an unknown state to within $\textit{multiplicative}$ error $\varepsilon$. When one can perform general collective measurements, $Θ\left(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d}{\varepsilon}\right)$ copies are known to be necessary and sufficient for this problem [AISW20]. However, implementing collective measurements on such a large number of copies can be experimentally demanding, and we thus aim to characterize the copy complexity of this problem with incoherent measurements.
  In this setting, the only non-trivial result is a non-adaptive algorithm that uses $O\left(
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon}
  \right)$ copies [PTTW26], which is polynomially larger than the best-known lower bound. Our first result is a new algorithm performing non-adaptive incoherent measurements that succeeds using $O\left(
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}
  \right)$ copies, improving on the latter term in the copy complexity. Moreover, we show that for any algorithm restricted to non-adaptive measurements, the above copy complexity is optimal.
  We also develop a new adaptive estimator for the purity of a state that improves on the above complexity in the high-precision regime, i.e., for $\varepsilon = o(1/d)$. We also show that the non-adaptive and adaptive estimators together yield the optimal complexity for incoherent purity estimation; in particular, we show that the copy complexity of this problem is $$
  Θ\left(\min\left\{
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}, \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon} + \frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2}\right\}
  \right).
  $$

</details>


### [An (almost) efficient classical algorithm for sampling from typical Gibbs states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40250)

Saleh Naghdi、Eric R. Anschuetz

量子算法 · 量子模拟 · 量子多体物理

**中文摘要**

Gibbs 态制备已成为在研究物理系统中获得量子优势的一条潜在途径。尽管对于最坏情况 Gibbs 态的局域性质计算已知存在优势，近期结果却表明平均情况局域系统呈现出不同图景：在其 Gibbs 态可被量子高效制备的温度下，准多项式时间经典算法也能够计算局域期望值。然而，这在更强的模拟概念——采样——下仍留下优势的可能性。Gibbs 采样是基于 Boltzmann 机的量子学习算法和量子霸权提案的基础，但其在物理相关模型中的经典平均情况复杂性仍未解决。

在这里，我们给出了强有力的证据，反对在一类称为量子 $p$-自旋模型的局域系统系综的 Gibbs 采样中存在指数级量子优势。我们描述了一种经典算法，可在总变差距离上以任意多项式小的误差从 Gibbs 态采样。

我们的算法基于一种称为算法随机局部化（algorithmic stochastic localization, ASL）的元算法。我们的主要技术结果是一个 ASL 的广义收敛定理，它把超立方体上任意分布的近似采样归约为将相关“倾斜分布”的均值估计到足够小的加性误差。

随后，我们将已知的、用于计算相关无序模型局域期望值的准多项式时间算法推广，以计算量子 $p$-自旋模型的这些“倾斜均值”。

最后，我们给出一个非严格的物理论证，说明该均值估计算法可一直工作到自旋玻璃转变温度。由于高效量子算法在该转变处也失效，这表明它们无法在低于经典算法同样（几乎）高效的温度下对量子 $p$-自旋模型进行 Gibbs 采样。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

提供的材料表明，作者提出一种准多项式时间经典算法，可在总变差距离多项式小误差下从量子 $p$-自旋模型的 Gibbs 态采样，并借助 ASL 收敛定理与倾斜均值估计把问题化为局域期望值计算；结合非严格物理论证，作者认为在经典算法同样（几乎）高效的温度范围内不存在指数级量子优势，且高效量子算法在自旋玻璃转变处也失效。结论主要适用于量子 $p$-自旋这类局域系统及静态性质的 Gibbs 采样；作者同时指出，局域观测估计或 Gibbs 采样中是否仍存在实质性多项式量子—经典分离仍是开放问题，并建议从静态性质的多项式分离或动力学性质的指数优势方向寻找量子优势。

**创新点**

- 提出一种基于算法随机局部化（ASL）的经典算法，用于从量子 $p$-自旋模型的 Gibbs 态进行采样，并达到总变差距离意义下多项式小的误差。
- 证明 ASL 的广义收敛定理，将超立方体上任意分布的近似采样归约为对相关倾斜分布均值的低加性误差估计。
- 将相关无序模型的准多项式时间局域期望值算法推广到量子 $p$-自旋模型的倾斜均值计算，并给出其可工作至自旋玻璃转变温度的非严格物理论证。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Gibbs state preparation has emerged as a potential avenue for quantum advantage in the study of physical systems. Although there is known advantage for computing local properties of worst-case Gibbs states, recent results suggest a different picture for average-case local systems: at temperatures where their Gibbs states can be efficiently prepared quantumly, quasi-polynomial time classical algorithms can also compute local expectation values. Yet this leaves open the possibility of an advantage under a stronger notion of simulation: sampling. Gibbs sampling underlies Boltzmann machine-based quantum learning algorithms and quantum supremacy proposals, but its classical average-case complexity for physically relevant models remains unresolved. Here, we give strong evidence against an exponential quantum advantage in Gibbs sampling for an ensemble of local systems known as the quantum $p$-spin model. We describe a classical algorithm for sampling from the Gibbs state to any polynomially small error in total variation distance. Our algorithm is based on a meta-algorithm known as algorithmic stochastic localization (ASL). Our main technical result is a generalized convergence theorem for ASL which reduces approximate sampling from any distribution on the hypercube to estimating the mean of a related "tilted distribution" to sufficiently low additive error. We then extend known quasi-polynomial time algorithms for computing local expectation values of a related disordered model to compute these "tilted means" for the quantum $p$-spin model. Finally, we give a non-rigorous physics argument that this mean-estimation algorithm works down to the spin glass transition temperature. As efficient quantum algorithms also fail at this transition, this suggests they cannot Gibbs sample the quantum $p$-spin model at temperatures below that for which classical algorithms are also (almost) efficient.

</details>


### [Quantum oblique eigenprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40254)

Alexander M. Dalzell、Yuan Su

量子算法 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

每个方阵将其底层希尔伯特空间分解为广义的、非正交的特征子空间。我们证明，在给定对输入矩阵的块编码访问时，量子计算机可以执行这样的斜特征投影 $Π$。我们的方法在输入满足谱集条件时，查询复杂度与逆间隙近乎线性，归一化因子接近 $\lVert\Pi\rVert$。这涵盖了关于数值域或可对角化的常见假设，并与正交特征投影的已知结果相匹配。我们通过双侧块预处理实现这一点，该预处理使用矩阵预解式的离散傅里叶变换。我们描述了应用于：(i) 准备具有复特征值的矩阵的特征态，将 Low 和 Su 的量子特征值变换算法拓展到实谱之外；(ii) 求解连续时间代数 Riccati 方程，相较于 Rodenas-Ruiz、Zhao 和 Lee 先前的求解器实现三次加速；以及 (iii) 求解普通 Sylvester 方程，相较于 Wang 和 Liu 的直接增广方法实现二次改进。我们的结果为实现非解析矩阵函数在量子计算机上的应用提示了一条有前景的途径。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

所提供的结论节选表明，在输入矩阵为非正规且可通过块编码访问、并满足谱集条件时，作者的方法能以对逆间隙近乎线性的查询复杂度和接近最优的归一化因子实现斜特征投影；该结果改进了标准预解积分方法，并用于复特征值矩阵的特征态制备、连续时间代数 Riccati 方程和 Sylvester 方程求解，使近期算法对间隙的依赖改善二次至三次因子。适用范围受谱集条件、非正规输入和块编码访问假设约束。

**创新点**

- 提出一种在块编码访问下执行斜特征投影的量子算法，其查询复杂度对逆间隙近乎线性，归一化因子接近最优值。
- 通过完整刻画标准预解积分生成的块编码，并利用矩阵预解式的离散傅里叶变换实现双侧块预处理。
- 将算法应用于复特征值矩阵的特征态制备、连续时间代数 Riccati 方程和 Sylvester 方程求解，并报告对近期算法间隙依赖的二次至三次改善。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Every square matrix decomposes its underlying Hilbert space into generalized, nonorthogonal eigensubspaces. We show that a quantum computer can perform such an oblique eigenprojection $Π$ given block encoding access to the input matrix. Our approach has a query complexity nearly linear in the inverse gap and a normalization factor close to $\lVertΠ\rVert$ under a spectral-set condition on the input. This covers common assumptions on the numerical range or diagonalizability and matches known results for orthogonal eigenprojections. We achieve this with a two-sided block preconditioning that uses a discrete Fourier transform of the matrix resolvent. We describe applications to: (i) preparing eigenstates of matrices with complex eigenvalues, extending the quantum eigenvalue transformation algorithm of Low and Su beyond real spectra; (ii) solving continuous-time algebraic Riccati equations, cubically speeding up a prior solver of Rodenas-Ruiz, Zhao, and Lee; and (iii) solving ordinary Sylvester equations, quadratically improving a direct augmented method of Wang and Liu. Our result suggests a promising route to applying nonanalytic matrix functions on quantum computers.

</details>


### [A General Theory of Multi-Resource Theories Involving Finite-Group Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40255)

Yosuke Mitsuhashi、Hiroyasu Tajima

量子信息理论 · 量子纠缠 · 量子热力学

**中文摘要**

资源理论提供了一个通用框架，用于量化量子态在量子科学中遇到的各种约束下作为资源的性质，例如纠缠、magic、非热性和不对称性。由于现实设定通常同时施加多个约束，研究将这些约束组合起来的多资源理论十分重要。其中，与不对称性的组合尤其自然，因为对称性是物理学中基础且普遍存在的。在这里，我们发展了一种通用理论，以统一方式处理有限群不对称性与广泛资源理论的组合。我们的关键观察是：组合两种约束的效果由底层资源理论下对称信息的可获取性决定：转换所需的信息能否从输入中提取并用于控制自由操作。如果这能以渐近消失的误差做到，我们证明组合理论的转换速率等于两个组成理论速率中的较小者，因此组合它们不会带来额外代价。该条件对任意混合多体态上的LOCC、Clifford对称性下多量子比特系统的magic态转换以及Gibbs保持操作成立。热操作由于具有时间平移协变性，只能获取有限的对称信息，从而给出两种对称性之间的额外兼容性条件。我们还分析了施加在基本操作而非仅施加在所得信道上的对称性。我们在适当假设下为LOCC和热操作建立了类似结果，并在对称性由Pauli算符表示时对magic建立了类似结果。最后，我们证明有限群对称性不会降低每副本的渐近ergotropy，因此不会引入新的完全被动态。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

该工作表明，有限群对称性约束在纠缠、magic和热力学资源理论中呈现共同结构：当对称信息可由亚线性数量的副本提取并用于条件控制，且转换满足相应兼容性条件时，映射层面的渐近转换速率可保持不变；若 Sym(ρ) ⊄ Sym(σ)，则在G协变操作下不可能有正的渐近转换速率。作者还指出，速率保持不意味着有限群约束在操作上平凡：有限副本下判别步骤会消耗副本并引入误差，因此仍可能对对称性敏感；有限群假设对上述机制是关键，连续群不能直接套用。结论的适用范围限于所讨论的LOCC、稳定子相关操作、Gibbs保持操作和热操作等类别及相应假设。

**创新点**

- 作者提出统一处理有限群不对称性与多类资源理论组合的通用框架，并给出在对称信息可渐近无误获取时组合转换速率等于两个组成理论速率较小者的定理。
- 作者证明有限群对称性下存在精确阻碍（Sym(ρ)⊄Sym(σ)时无正渐近速率），并在LOCC、Clifford对称下的magic转换、Gibbs保持操作和热操作等设定中给出速率保持或兼容性条件，同时区分映射层面与基本操作层面的约束。
- 作者证明有限群对称性不降低每副本渐近ergotropy，也不扩大完全被动态集合。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Resource theories provide a universal framework for quantifying properties of quantum states, such as entanglement, magic, athermality, and asymmetry, as resources under various constraints encountered in quantum science. Since realistic settings typically impose several constraints at once, it is important to study multi-resource theories that combine them. Among these, combinations with asymmetry are especially natural, as symmetry is fundamental and ubiquitous in physics. Here we develop a general theory that treats combinations of finite-group asymmetry with a broad range of resource theories in a unified manner. Our key observation is that the effect of combining the two constraints is governed by the accessibility of symmetry information under the underlying resource theory: whether the information needed for the conversion can be extracted from the input and used to control free operations. If this can be done with asymptotically vanishing error, we show that the conversion rate of the combined theory equals the smaller of the rates of the two constituent theories, so combining them incurs no additional cost. This condition holds for LOCC on arbitrary mixed multipartite states, magic-state conversion of multiqubit systems under Clifford symmetries, and Gibbs-preserving operations. Thermal operations, being time-translation covariant, can access only limited symmetry information, giving an additional compatibility condition between the two symmetries. We also analyze symmetry imposed on elementary operations rather than only on the resulting channel. We establish analogous results for LOCC and thermal operations under suitable assumptions, and for magic when the symmetry is represented by Pauli operators. Finally, we show that finite-group symmetry does not reduce the asymptotic ergotropy per copy and hence introduces no new completely passive states.

</details>


### [Self-testing the toric code against classical communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40259)

Gustavo Fróes、Amanda Wei、Carlos de Gois、Denis Rochette、Marc-Olivier Renou

量子纠错 · 量子网络 · 量子信息理论

**中文摘要**

设备无关自检验仅凭多方量子态所产生的关联即可认证该量子态，而不需要对设备内部工作机制作任何假设。然而，现有大多数自检验一旦各方能够交换消息就会失效。我们考虑量子网络中的一种放宽设定：在协议的诚实版本中，各方从不通信；但即使在不信任的情形下，不信任设备在网络中有限距离内秘密交换经典消息，认证仍然保持可靠性。我们证明，分布在具有环面几何结构的网络上的环面码码空间，可以在该设定下以噪声鲁棒的方式被自检验，并能抵御距离与网络直径成比例的通信。我们还发展了一种用于构造此类协议的算法方法，并将结果扩展到表面码和簇态，二者对一轮最近邻经典通信具有鲁棒性。

**研究结论（依据正文结论节选与摘要）**

作者表明，即使不信任设备在有界距离内秘密交换经典消息，量子纠错码子空间仍可被自检验；对于环面码，作者给出了噪声鲁棒的解析构造，可容忍各方在正比于晶格尺寸的距离上交换经典消息，并达到最优渐近标度。作者还给出基于整数线性规划的系统搜索，在较小环面码晶格上得到协议，并扩展到表面码和二维簇态，二者对最近邻经典通信具有鲁棒性。上述结论适用于所考虑的有界距离经典通信与噪声鲁棒设定；秘密量子通信等扩展未被实际处理。

**创新点**

- 作者提出环面码码空间的噪声鲁棒自检验解析构造，可容忍与晶格尺寸成比例的经典通信距离，并达到最优渐近标度。
- 作者引入基于整数线性规划的系统搜索方法，用于构造此类自检验协议，并在较小环面码晶格上得到协议。
- 作者将方法扩展到表面码和二维簇态，并报告了一个无需辅助方的6×6簇态直接自检验，对最近邻经典通信具有鲁棒性。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

Device-independent self-testing certifies a multipartite quantum state from the correlations it produces alone, without assumptions on the inner workings of the devices. Most existing self-tests, however, break down as soon as the parties can exchange messages. We consider a relaxed setting in a quantum network, where in the honest version of the protocol, the parties never communicate, yet certification remains sound even in an untrusted case where the untrusted devices secretly exchange classical messages within a bounded distance in the network. We show that the code space of the toric code, distributed over a network with the geometry of a torus, can be self-tested in this setting in a noise-robust way, against communication up to distances proportional to the network diameter. We also develop an algorithmic method for constructing such protocols and extend our results to the surface code and cluster states, robust to one round of nearest-neighbor classical communication

</details>


### [Quantum Algorithms for Minimum Generating Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40260)

Bireswar Das、Udit Kumar、Kavita Samant、Dhara Thakkar

量子算法 · 量子复杂性

**中文摘要**

本文提出一种多项式时间量子算法，用于计算可解黑盒群的最小规模生成集。接下来，我们考虑黑盒群类 $\Gamma_d$，其中每个非阿贝尔合成因子同构于固定 $d$ 下对称群 $S_d$ 的一个子群。我们设计多项式时间量子算法，以计算阿贝尔因子群的直积分解，并解决来自 $\Gamma_d$ 的群的因子群的构造性成员问题。借助这些算法，我们设计了一种量子算法，用于计算来自 $\Gamma_d$ 的黑盒群的主列。利用主列，我们构造了一个多项式时间量子算法，用于计算来自 $\Gamma_d$ 的黑盒群的最小生成集。最后，我们证明一般黑盒群的最小生成集问题属于 $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$。

**研究结论（依据摘要）**

仅依据摘要，作者给出的结论是：对于可解黑盒群，可以多项式时间量子计算最小规模生成集；对于每个非阿贝尔合成因子都同构于固定 $d$ 下 $S_d$ 的子群的黑盒群类 $\Gamma_d$，可借助阿贝尔因子群直积分解、构造性成员问题和主列的多项式时间量子算法来计算最小生成集；一般黑盒群的最小生成集问题属于 $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$。这些结论的适用范围限于摘要所述的黑盒群框架及相应群类，且未提供正文结论章节。

**创新点**

- 提出计算可解黑盒群最小规模生成集的多项式时间量子算法。
- 针对黑盒群类 $\Gamma_d$，设计计算阿贝尔因子群直积分解、求解构造性成员问题并计算主列的多项式时间量子算法，进而计算最小生成集。
- 证明一般黑盒群的最小生成集问题属于 $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$。

<details>
<summary>原文摘要</summary>

In this paper, we present a polynomial-time quantum algorithm for computing a minimum-sized generating set of solvable black-box groups. Next, we consider the class $Γ_d$ of black-box groups, where every non-abelian composition factor is isomorphic to a subgroup of the symmetric group $S_d$ for a fixed $d$. We design polynomial-time quantum algorithms to compute the direct product decomposition of abelian factor groups and solve the constructive membership problem for factor groups of groups from $Γ_d$. With the help of these algorithms, we design a quantum algorithm for computing a chief series of black-box groups from $Γ_d$. Using the chief series, we construct a polynomial-time quantum algorithm for computing minimum generating sets of black-box groups from $Γ_d$.
